SB 10.16

(10.16)

atha ṣoḍaśo’dhyāyaḥ

kāliya-damanam—nāgapatnī-kṛtaṁ nāga-kartṛkaṁ ca bhagavataḥ stavanaṁ nāga-dvārā hrada-parityāgaś ca |

|| 10.16.1 ||

śrī-śuka uvāca

vilokya dūṣitāṁ kṛṣṇāṁ kṛṣṇaḥ kṛṣṇāhinā vibhuḥ |

tasyā viśuddhim anvicchan sarpaṁ tam udavāsayat ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) :

ṣoḍaśe kāliyasyokto nigraho yamunā-hrade |

tat-patnībhiḥ stutenātha kṛṣṇenānugrahaḥ kṛtaḥ ||

hatvā rāsabha-daiteyān jagdhvā tāla-phalāny alam |

prīto’nṛtyat phaṇā-raṅge kāliyasya kalānidhiḥ ||

udavāsayan niḥsāritavān ||1||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : atha śruti-proktaṁ kāliya-damanam āha—yo'hann ahim anvapas tatardety apāhasto apṛtanyad indram [ṛ.ve. 1.32.36.1] iti | arthas tu—yo’hiḥ sarpo’po yanunā-jalāni tatarda viṣa-samparkeṇa nāśitavān, tam indro’han hiṁsitavān pīḍana-mātram atra hanty arthaḥ | sa cāhir apāt pāda-hīnaḥ ahastaḥ śvasan indram apṛtanyat ayodhayata | iyam evāhi-rūpatvād vṛtraya tad vadham api āha | atrendraḥ kṛṣṇas tatra vāsava iti |

nigraho daṇḍaḥ | tat-patnībhiḥ kāliya-bhāryābhiḥ |*|

jagdhvā bhuktvā | alaṁ prīto’tiprītaḥ | phaṇā-raṅge sphaṭā-rūpa-krīḍā-bhūmau | kalānāṁ nṛtyādīnāṁ vakṣyamāṇānāṁ nidhir ādhāraḥ |*|

śrī-śuka iha śrī-padena śuko yathājñātam eva vadati tathā so’pi vraja-jana-duḥkhānusandhānena vistārya novāca rājñaḥ śrī-kṛṣṇāpriyatvāt | kṛṣṇāhinā śyāma-sarpeṇa | kṛṣṇām yamunām kṛṣṇā syād draupadī nīlī kālindī mariceṣu ca | kaṇā-drākṣāsu bhadrāsu iti dharaṇiḥ | tasyāḥ kṛṣṇāyāḥ | viśuddhim nirviṣa-toyatām ||1||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : vilokya dūṣitām ity-ādi | kṛṣṇāṁ yamunāṁ kṛṣṇāhinā dūṣitāṁ vilokya tasyā viśuddhim anvicchan kṛṣṇāhim udavāsayat | ayaṁ bhāvaḥ—svayaṁ kṛṣṇaḥ kṛṣṇeti nāmnā mitra-bhūtāṁ katham anyo dūṣayiṣyati ? mitrabhūtāpy eṣā śodhayitavyeti sva-nāmnā mitram api**khalaś ca daṇḍanīya eveti tathā dvayaṁ kṛtavān ||1||

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : kṛṣṇam iti varṇato nāmataś cātaḥ sakhyās tasyā durviṣa-dūṣitatvenāvaśya-pratīkāryatvam abhipretam | kṛṣṇenāhina iti mahā-durviṣamayatvam uktam | kṛṣṇo jagad-dhitārtham eva svayam avatīrṇo bhagavān ity arthaḥ | jala-viśodhanena hi tasyās tena jala-pānādi-siddhyā vrajasya ca, mahā-tīrthoddharaṇādinā ca jagatām api, tathā damanādinā kāliyasyāpi hitācaraṇāt | etat sarvam agre vyaktaṁ bhāvi | yato vibhur ekayāpi kriyayāneka-kāryaṁ kartuṁ samarthaḥ | ata eva sarpatīti sarpas tam iti tasyānyatra prayāṇa-prayāso'pi nirastaḥ ||1||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : kṛṣṇām iti | varṇato nāmataś ca, ataḥ sakhyās tasyā durviṣa-dūṣitatvenāvaśya-pratīkāryam abhipretam | kṛṣṇenāhina iti mahā-durviṣamayatvam uktaṁ | na kevalaṁ tasyā eva tad-dhitāyābhūt, api tu sarveṣām api, yataḥ kṛṣṇaḥ karṣati duḥkhānīti jala-pānādi-siddhyā vrajasya, mahā-tīrthoddhāraṇādinā jagatām api, damanādinā kāliyasyāpi hitācaraṇāt bālya-līlā-rasāviṣṭasyāpi tasya pūrvavat svāvasareṇa aiśvaryam api udayate ity āha—vibhur iti ||1||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : atha kāliya-damana-līlāṁ pravaktum upakramamāṇa āha—vilokya dūṣitām ity-ādi | kṛṣṇaḥ kṛṣṇāhinā dūṣitāṁ kṛṣṇaṁ yamunāṁ vilokya tasyā viśuddhiṁ viśeṣeṇa śuddhim anvicchan taṁ sarpam udavāsayad iti vākyārthaḥ | tātparyārthas tu svayaṁ śrī-kṛṣṇaḥ sva-nāmnā mitra-bhūtāṁ katham anyo dūṣayiṣyati, tata enāṁ śodhayiṣya iti mitra-vātsalyam, eka-deśena suhṛd api yadi khalaḥ syāt, tadā so’pi daṇḍanīya eva, tatra mitratvenoparodho na kārya iti sad-upadeśaṁ ca darśayituṁ tathā kṛṣṇaḥ kṛṣṇāṁ kṛṣṇāhinety anuprāsa-dhvaniḥ ||1||

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) :

nyagṛhṇāt kāliyaṁ kṛṣṇo darśayan svam apād vrajam |

stuto’hibhiḥ prasannas tān ṣoḍaśe nirasārayat ||

kṛṣṇāṁ yamunām udavāsayat tasmān niḥsāritavān ||1||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) :

om namaḥ kāliya-mardanāya kṛṣṇāya

ṣoḍaśe kāliyaṁ kṛṣṇo nyagṛhṇāt tad-vadhas tataḥ |

anugṛhya tam utsārya yamunāṁ nirviṣāṁ vyadhāt ||

kṛṣṇāṁ yamunāṁ nāmato varṇataś ca sāmyāt sakhīm avaśya-kartavyopakārām iti bhāvaḥ | kṛṣṇāhinā kāliya-sarpeṇa | udavāsayat niḥsāritavān ||1||

...

|| 10.16.2 ||

rājovāca—

katham antar-jale’gādhe nyagṛhṇād bhagavān ahim |

sa vai bahu-yugāvāsaṁ yathāsīd vipra kathyatām ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : bahūni yugāny āvaso yasya tam ahim | sa vai ahir ajala-caro’pi tasminn antar-jale yathā yena prakāreṇa āsīt | athavā, bahūni yugāny āvāso yathā bhavati, tathā yena prakāreṇa sa vai tatrāsīd iti kathyatām iti ||2||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : saḥ kāliyaḥ | agādhe tala-sparśe | sarpa-viśeṣaṇatve tv ajalacarasya jale ciraṁ sthity-asambhavāt prakārāntaram āha—athaveti | yathā yena prakāreṇa bahu-yugāvāsaṁ bahu-yugāny āvāso yatra tad bahu-yugāvāsaṁ syāt, tathetīhārthena gṛhṇāti—yena prakāreṇa iti | tatra kṛṣṇāyām | āsīd babhūva | iti ko'rthaḥ sa prakāraḥ kathyatām | he vipra ! parama-vidyā-pravīṇatvāt tvaṁ sarvaṁ jānāsīti bhāvaḥ |

janmanā brāhmaṇo jñeyaḥ saṁskārair dvija ucyate |

vidyayā yāti vipratvaṁ tribhiḥ śrotriya-lakṣaṇam || iti yājñavalkyāt ||2||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : vai ca—sa ca | bahu-yugāvāsam iti dūṣitām ity ukteḥ | kāliya-hṛda iti prasiddher bālya-kāla-vāsena tad-asambhavāt | bhagavān mādṛśāṁ prabhur iti praharṣāt | kiṁ vā, agādha-jalāntar-duṣṭāhi-nigrahe duḥkha-śaṅkayā śokād eva, ato viśeṣeṇa prakarṣeṇa ca kathyatām | yad vā, viśeṣataḥ prāti sarva-manorathaṁ pūrayatīti vipraḥ, tasya sambodhanam, tat-kathanena mādṛśāṁ kāmaṁ paripūrayeti bhāvaḥ ||2||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : bhagavān sarveṇāpi prakāreṇa kartuṁ samarthaḥ tathāpi kathaṁ kena prakāreṇeti vai ca sa ca bahu-yugāvāsam iti viśrutatvāt bahūni yugāni āvāso yathā tādṛśaṁ yathā bhavati tathā yathā āsīt tat kathyatām ity evānvayaḥ | vipra ! he parama-vidyā-pravīṇa ! tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ—

janmanā brāhmaṇo jñeyaḥ saṁskārair dvija ucyate |

vidyayā yāti vipratvaṁ tribhiḥ śrotriya-lakṣaṇam || iti ||2||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : bahu-yugāvāsam iti viśrutatvāt bahūni yugāni āvāso yatra tādṛśaṁ yathā bhavati, tathā yathā syāt tathā kathyatām ity evānvayaḥ ||2||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : bahūni yugāni vyāpya āvāso yatra tad yathā syāt, tathā āsīt | viśeṣataḥ prakarṣeṇa kathyatām ||2||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : agādhe jale’ntaḥ katham ahiṁ nyagṛhṇāt sa vai sa cāhir ajala-caro’pi bahūni yugany āvāso yatra tad yathā syāt, tathā tasmin jale katham āsīt kathyatām | he vipra ! vidyāti-nipuṇa !

janmanā brahmaṇo jñeyaḥ saṁskārair dvija ucyate |

vidyayā yāti vipratvaṁ tribhiḥ śrotriya-lakṣaṇām || iti-yājñavalkyokteḥ ||2||

...

|| 10.16.3 ||

brahma bhagavatas tasya bhūmnaḥ svacchanda-vartinaḥ |

gopālodāra-caritaṁ kas tṛpyetāmṛtaṁ juṣan ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : gopālena yad udāraṁ caritam ācaritaṁ, tad evāmṛtam | ataḥ kathyatām iti ||3||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : he brahman ! iti tvaṁ sākṣād-brahma-rūpatvenānya-prabodhane’pi samartho’sīti bhāvaḥ | evaṁ śleṣeṇa sarva-bhakta-śrotrādīndriyāṇāṁ pālanena udāraṁ sukha-dātṛ caritam iti | svānāṁ svasya vā chandena vartituṁ śīlam asyeti tathā tasya | juṣan sevamānaḥ ||3||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : gopālodāra-caritam iti | gopālavad udāraṁ sundaraṁ yac caritaṁ tad eva amṛtam ||3||

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : nanu, tad viśeṣa-kathanaṁ parama-duḥkha-pradam, ata eva mayā saṅkṣipya kathitam, tatrāha—brahmann iti | bhagavataḥ sarvaiśvarya-yuktatvād eva bhūmnaḥ paripūrṇasya svacchanda-vartinaś ca parama-svatantrasya tādṛśasyāpi gopālana-rūpam ata evodāraṁ sarvotkṛṣṭaṁ sukha-pradaṁ vā yac caritam amṛtam iti maraṇa-tulya-mahā-duḥkha-nivartakam, tad eva mahauṣadham iti bhāvaḥ |

yad vā, gopālanenodāra-caritatvasya hetutvena trīṇi viśeṣaṇāni—tatra bhagavata iti tenaiva bhagavato viśeṣa-prakaṭanāt, ata eva bhūmno'paricchanna-māhātmyasya, yataḥ svānāṁ chandena icchayā vartituṁ śīlasya,

yad vā, tattva-dṛṣṭyā tad-duḥkhaṁ sahyam api syād iti tribhir viśeṣaṇair āha—tatrādyābhyāṁ vakṣyamāṇāvasthādikaṁ nirastam, ātmecchayā vartina ity anena ca mahā-kautukinas tasya līlayā kautukam eva, na tu śoka iti |

yad vā, bhaktecchānuvartina iti tasya sadā sarvatra sukham eveti bodhitam | athavā, amṛtam iti parama-duḥkhamayasyāpi tac-caritasya tasya prema-viśeṣa-svabhāvavat paramānandamayatvam evābhipretam | tac ca śrī-bhāgavatāmṛtottara-khaṇḍe vivṛtam asti | gopāleti sambodhanam |

yad vā, he vākpate ! kiṁ vā, śrī-bhagavatā saha guror abheda-dṛṣṭyā, he śrī-kṛṣṇety arthaḥ | anyat samānam | atas tasmād uparantuṁ śaknomi, kiṁ kuryām ataḥ kathyatām eveti bhāvaḥ | brahman he vedamayeti yathā vedeṣu vartamānaṁ duḥkha-vratam api na kila tyajyate, tathedam api na tyājyaṁ syād iti bhavatā jñāyata eveti bhāvaḥ ||3||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : yady api śrī-munīndreṇa tad viśeṣa-duḥkha-śaṅkayā saṅkṣipya kathitaṁ, tathāpi tad-ajñānena rājñā tal-līlā-sāmānyābhilāṣād eva yathā dṛṣṭam iti tad vākyenaiva darśayati—brahmann iti | bhagavataḥ aiśvaryādi-ṣaṭka-yuktasya bhūmnaḥ sarvathāpi sarvātiśayitasya tathāpi svacchanda-vartinaḥ svaira-līlā-prakāśinaḥ, evam evambhūtasya gopāla-jāty-anurūpam udāraṁ sarvato’pi mahat paramānanda-dātṛ vā yac caritaṁ taj juṣamāṇaḥ kas tṛpyet ? atṛptau hetuḥ—amṛtam iveti ||3||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : yadyapi munīndreṇa tad-viśeṣa-duḥkha-śaṅkayā saṅkṣipya kathitaṁ, tathā tad-ajñānena vā tal-līlā-sāmānyābhilāṣād eva tathā pṛṣṭam iti darśayati—brahmann iti | sa tu tat-prītaye sva-duḥkha-kathā-nivedanāya ca nātyantam arthe’bhiniveśya yathā-śrutam evovācety arthaḥ ||3.4||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : atha tat-keli-śravaṇa-śraddhālutāṁ rājā prakaṭayann āha—brahmann ity-ādi | he brahman bhūmnaḥ parāsarasya tasyācintya-parameśvaryasya bhagavataḥ kṛṣṇasya svacchanda-vartinaḥ svādhīnasya gopālodāra-caritaṁ juṣan kas tṛpyeta | atra kasyāpi yasya kasyacij janasyāpi tṛptir nāsti, tad-bandhu-pautrasya tad-bhāgineya-putrasya tenaiva rakṣitasya mama punas tṛptir na syāt, tat kiṁ ?

ayaṁ bhāvaḥ—tathā-vidheśvarasya tathā-vidhaiśvarya-pratipādakeṣu cariteṣu varaṁ tṛptir jāyate, vaicitryābhāvāt, tasya tu gopāla-krīḍāyāṁ vaicitrya-viśeṣavat tayā na tṛptir iti gopālodāra-śabdayor mahimā ||4.8||

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : gavāṁ śleṣeṇa sarva-bhakta-śrotrādīndriyāṇāṁ ca pālanenodāraṁ sukha-dātṛ-caritam ||3||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : gavām paśūnāṁ sva-bhakta-śrotrendriyāṇāṁ ca pālanenodāraṁ sukha-dātṛ-caritam ||3||

...

|| 10.16.4 ||

śrī-śuka uvāca

kālindyāṁ kāliyasyāsīd dhradaḥ kaścid viṣāgninā |

śrapyamāṇa-payā yasmin patanty uparigāḥ khagāḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : śrapyamāṇaṁ pacyamānaṁ payo yasya saḥ | ata upari gacchantaḥ khagā yasmin patanti ||4||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : kālindyā hradaḥ iti harivaṁśokter yojana-pramāṇas tasyā dakṣiṇa-bhāge vartamānas tad-gargajas tat-pravāhāspṛṣṭa eva, anyathā tad viṣa-saṁpṛkta-jalavatī sā mathurādi-deśastha-janair avyavahāryevābhaviṣyad iti jñeyam | hradaḥ agādha-jalavad-vaṭa-viśeṣaḥ | tatrāgādha-jalo hradaḥ ity amaraḥ ||4||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : kālindyāṁ hrada iti tasyā dakṣiṇa-bhāge vartamānasya tasya tayā saha saṅgamenaikya-prāptes tasyāḥ pravāhas tūttarata evohyo'nyathā tad-durviṣa-jalena yādava-kulāvāsa-śrī-madhu-puryādi-vyāpteḥ | āsīd ity atīta-nirdeśa-kāliya-sambandhādy-apekṣayā, kaścid eko'nirvacanīyo vā | anirvacanīyatvam eva darśayati—viṣety-ādi | uparigā ūrdhve gacchantaḥ khagā iti, tatrāpi dūragā ity abhipretam ||4||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : śrī-śuka uvāceti tad evaṁ niśamya savāsanatve tasmin jāta-snehaḥ sukha-duḥkhātmakaṁ sarvam eva tādṛśāya nivedanīyam ity abhiprāyeṇa śrī-vraja-vāsi-duḥkhamaya-nija-duḥkhātmakam api tan nivedayan śrī-śuka-vad yathāśrutam evovāca, na tv anyatraiva vistāryāpīty arthaḥ | kālindyāṁ hrada iti tasyā dakṣiṇa-bhāge vartamānasya tasya tad-garbhajatvāt tasyāḥ pravāhas tūttarataḥ pṛthag evohyaḥ, anyathā tad-durviṣa-jalena yādava-kula-vāsa-śrī-madhupury-ādi-vyāpteḥ | sa tu kāliyasya viṣāgninā śrapyamāṇa-payā āsīt uparigāḥ ūrdhvaṁ gacchantaḥ khagā iti, tatrāpi dūragatvam abhipretam ||4||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, jīva-gosvāmī bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : kālindyāṁ hrada iti hari-vaṁśokta-yojana-pramāṇaḥ, tasyā dakṣiṇe bhāge tat-pravāheṇāspṛṣṭa eva | anyathā tad-viṣa-saṁpṛkta-pravāhavatī sā mathurādi-deśastha-janair avyavahāryaivābhaviṣyad iti jñeyam | śrapyamāṇaṁ pacyamānaṁ payo yasya saḥ ||4||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : kālindyām iti | tasyā dakṣiṇe bhāge tat-pravāheṇāspṛṣṭo yojana-pramāṇo hradaḥ śrī-harivaṁśokter bodhyaḥ tat-sparśe tat-pravāhasya māthurādibhir avyavāryatā syāt | śrapyamānaṁ pacyamānaṁ payo jalaṁ yasya saḥ | patanti viṣa-jvālayā viglātāḥ santaḥ ||4||

...

|| 10.16.5 ||

vipruṣmatā viṣodormi-mārutenābhimarśitāḥ |

mriyante tīragā yasya prāṇinaḥ sthira-jaṅgamāḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : kiṁ ca, vipruṣmatā ambu-kaṇa-yuktena viṣoda-taraṅga-sparśi-mārutena spṛṣṭā yasya tīragā mriyante, sa hrada āsīd iti ||5||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : atrānyad āha—kiṁ ca, iti | yasya hradasya | abhimarśitāḥ spṛṣṭāḥ ||5||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : vipruṣmateti śrapyamāṇa-payas tayā sadā javena tat-phaṇānām uccalanād iti mārutasya atidāhakatvam uktam | anyaś ca viśeṣaḥ śrī-hari-vaṁśe—

dīrgha yojana-vistāraṁ dustaraṁ tri-daśair api |

gambhīra-makṣobhya-jalaṁ niṣkampam iva sāgaram ||

duḥkhopasarpaṁ tīreṣu sasarpair vipulair bilaiḥ |

viṣāraṇibhavasyāgner dhūmena pariveṣṭitam ||

tṛṇeṣv api patatsv apsu jvalantam iva tejasā |

samantād yojanaṁ sāgraṁ tīreṣv api durāsadam || [ha.vaṁ. 2.11.42,44,46] iti ||5||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : vipruṣmateti | śrapyamāṇa-payas tayā sadā javena tat-kāraṇānām uccalanāt iti mārutasyāpi dāhakatvam uktam | anyaś ca viśeṣaḥ śrī-harivaṁśe—

dīrghaṁ yojana-vistāram agamyaṁ tridaśair api |

gambhīram akṣobhya-jalaṁ niṣkampam iva sāgaram ||

duḥkhopasarpaṁ tīreṣu sasarpair vipulair vilaiḥ |

viṣāraṇībhavasyāgner dhūmena pariveṣṭitam ||

tṛṇeṣv api patatsv apsu jvalantam iva tejasā |

samantād yojanaṁ sāgraṁ tīreṣv api durāsadam || [ha.vaṁ. 2.11.42,44,46] iti |

evaṁ bhūmi-guhāyāṁ tat-purī-jala-stambha-vidyayā jala-madhya eva vā ||5||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : vipruṣmateti jala-stambha-vidyayā tan-madhya eva tat-purī duḥkhopasarpaṁ tīreṣu sa-sarpair vipulair bilaiḥ [ha.vaṁ. 2. 42] iti śrī-hari-vaṁśa-dṛṣṭyā bileṣv api ||5.6||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : vipruṣmatā ambu-kaṇā-yuktena viṣodaka-taraṅga-sparśi-mārutena abhimṛṣṭāḥ spṛṣṭāḥ ||5 ||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : vipruṣmatā tad-ambu-kaṇa-yuktena abhimarṣitāḥ spṛṣṭāḥ santaḥ ||5||

...

|| 10.16.6 ||

taṁ caṇḍa-vega-viṣa-vīryam avekṣya tena

duṣṭāṁ nadīṁ ca khala-saṁyamanāvatāraḥ |

kṛṣṇaḥ kadambam adhiruhya tato’tituṅgam

āsphoṭya gāḍha-raśano nyapatad viṣode ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : taṁ kāliyam | caṇḍo vego yasya tad viṣam eva vīryaṁ yasya tam | kadambam iti | bhāvinā śrī-kṛṣṇa-caraṇa-sparśa-bhāgyena sa ekas tat-tīre na śuṣkaḥ iti jñātavyam | amṛtam āharatā garut-matā krāntatvād iti ca purāṇāntaram | āsphoṭya bāhuṁ kara-talenāhatya | gāḍhā dṛḍhaṁ baddhā raśanā kaṭi-bandhana-vastraṁ yena saḥ ||6||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : caṇḍas tīkṣṇo vegaḥ | tena kāliyena | tataḥ kadambāt | atituṅgāt atyuccāt | hrda iti śeṣaḥ ||6||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : vegas tejo javo vā, caṇḍety apratikāryatvādito'yaṁ niḥsāryaṁ eveti bhāvaḥ | tena kāliyena hetunā | adhirūhya tac chikharam ārūhya, tathā ca tatraiva arohac capalaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ kadamba-śikharaṁ mudā [hari-vaṁśe viṣṇu.pa. 2.12.1] iti | tataḥ kadambāt, gāḍha-rasanaḥ ity anena keśādīnām api gāḍha-bandhanam upalakṣyate, tathā ca tatraiva baddhvā parikaraṁ dṛḍham [hari-vaṁśe.viṣṇu.pa. 2.12.1] ity abhimukham apatat, nyapatat iti pāṭhe mahā-vegena kurditvā madhye'patat | yataḥ khalānāṁ duṣṭānāṁ saṁyamanāyāvatāraḥ śrī-vaikuṇṭhād avataraṇam, | kiṁ vā, matsyādy avatārā api yasya sa iti kāliya-damanārtham ity arthaḥ ||6||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : vegaḥ śīghra-prasarpaṇaṁ caṇḍeti apratikāryatvād itio’yaṁ niḥsārya eveti bhāvaḥ | tena kāliyena hetunā adhiruhya tac chikhare āruhya tathā ca tatraiva arohac capalaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ kadamba-śikharaṁ mudā [hari-vaṁśe viṣṇu.pa. 2.12.1] iti tataḥ kadambāt gāḍha-rasana ity anena keśādīnām api gāḍha-bandhanam upalakṣyate tathā ca tatraiva baddhvā parikaraṁ dṛḍham [hari-vaṁśe.viṣṇu.pa. 2.12.1] iti nyapatad ity atra hetuḥ khalānāṁ saṁyamanāya avatāraḥ sva-lokād avataraṇaṁ kiṁ vā, matsyādy avatārā api yasya saḥ iti kāliya-damanārtham ity arthaḥ | ekaḥ kadambaḥ kim avaśiṣṭaḥ śrī-kṛṣṇa-vṛkṣatvāt kṛṣṇavat

kadambaḥ kṛṣṇa-vṛkṣo hi kāliya-hṛda-samīpagaḥ |

tasmād eko na śuṣko’sau viṣa-hārakaraḥ parān ||

iti prasiddhyā śrī-kṛṣṇenaiva sa vihārāya rakṣita iti gamyate, parān iti vacanāt tat sannivāsino vṛkṣa-gaṇā api na mṛtā ity āyātam evety arthaḥ ||6||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : taṁ kāliyaṁ kadambam iti bhāvinā śrī-kṛṣṇa-caraṇa-sparśa-bhāgyena sa ekas tat-tīre na śuṣkaḥ amṛtam āharatā garutmatākrāntatvād iti purāṇāntaram iti śrī-svāmi-caraṇā gāḍhaṁ dṛḍhaṁ baddhā rasanā rasanāpadopalakṣitāḥ kuntaloṣṇīṣādayo’pi yena saḥ āsphoṭya bāhuṁ karatalenāhatya ||6||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : tam arhi kadambam adhiruhyātituṅgāt tato viṣode’bhyapatat | eṣa kadambo bhāvi-bhagavat pada-sparśa saubhāgyān na śuṣkaḥ sudhām āharatā garuḍena tasminn upaveśād vā, gāḍhā dṛḍha-baddhā rasanā parikara-bandhanaṁ yeneti kuntalīṣṇīṣāder upalakṣaṇam | bāhuṁ vāmaṁ dakṣiṇa-pāṇi-talenāsphoṭya āhatya ||6||

...

|| 10.16.7 ||

sarpa-hradaḥ puruṣa-sāra-nipāta-vega-

saṅkṣobhitoraga-viṣocchvasitāmbu-rāśiḥ |

paryak-pluto viṣa-kaṣāya-vibhīṣaṇormir

dhāvan dhanuḥ-śatam ananta-balasya kiṁ tat ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : tadā sarpasya hradaḥ | puruṣa-śreṣṭhasya patana-bhāreṇa saṅkṣobhitānām uragāṇāṁ viṣair unnato’mbu-rāśir yasya saḥ | viṣeṇa kaṣāyī-kṛtā bhayaṁ-karā ūrmayo yasya saḥ | paryak parito dhāvan dhanuḥ-śataṁ plutaḥ prasṛtaḥ | naitac citram ity āha—ananta-balasyeti ||7||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : tadā patana-kāle | kaṣāyī-kṛtāḥ kvathitāḥ | yad vā, viṣa-kaṣāyeṇa viṣa-rāgeṇa vibhīṣaṇā urmayo yasyeti kvarthe rāge kaṣāyostrī niryāse saurabhe rase iti yādavaḥ | kiṁ tat na kim apīty arthaḥ | lakṣa-yojana-vistārāyām aparīmita-bhū-plāvanam api tatra nādbhutāv aham iti bhāvaḥ | ṣaṇṇavaty aṅgulaṁ dhanuḥ ity amaraḥ | dhanur hasta-catuṣṭayam iti ca ||7||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : puruṣa-sāratvād eva vegena javenātiśīghraṁ sāṁkṣobhito'taevoraga-viṣocchavasitaś cāmbu-rāśir yasya saḥ | "kaṣāyita" iti pāṭhaḥ śrī-cit-sukhasya śrī-svāmi-pādānāṁ ca sammataḥ, kaṣāyī-kṛtāḥ iti vyākhyānāt | dhīman he vivekinn iti rājānam āśvāsayati | ananta bala śaktir yasya, tat karma | yad vā, anantasya nāgarājasyāpi bala yasmāt tasya tad viṣaṁ kim ? api tu na kiñcid apīty arthaḥ ||7||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : puruṣa-sāratvād eva nipāta-vegaḥ anyat taiḥ | yad vā, puruṣasya bhagavataḥ sāreṇa kiñcid bala-prākaṭyena yo nipātas tasya vegena javena saṁkṣobhitaḥ ata evoragaviṣoc cvasitaś cāmbu-rāśir yasya sa kaṣāyiteti pāṭhaḥ śrī-cit sukhasya śrī-svāmi-pādānāṁ ca sammataḥ kaṣāyī-kṛta iti vyākhyānāt | kaṣāyo’tra kvāthya-raso raktaḥ pīta-varṇo vā niryāse’pi kaṣāyo’trī ity atra kṣīra-svāminā tat tad vyākhyānāt | dhīman he vivekin ! iti rājānam āśvasayati, dhanuṣaḥ pramāṇam uktam—

aṣṭabhir yava-madhyaiḥ syād aṅgulaṁ dvādaśāṅgulam |

tālaṁ tritālako hasto hastau dvau kiṣkur ucyate ||

kiṣku-dvayaṁ dhanuḥ proktam |

ity ataḥ pūrvaṁ tāvat pradeśa-mātam ākramyaiva bālā gāvaś ca rakṣitā iti jñeyam anantaṁ balaṁ śaktir yasya tat karma | yad vā, anantasya nāga-rājasyāpi viṣādi-balaṁ yasmāt tasya tad viṣaṁ kim ? api tu na kiñcid apīty arthaḥ ||7||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : puruṣasya bhagavataḥ sāreṇa kiñcid bala-prākaṭyenety-ādy api grāhyaṁ kaṣāiteti pāṭhaḥ śrī-svāmī-cit-sukhasyāpi mataḥ kaṣāyī-kṛtā iti vyākhyānāt ||7.8||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : tataś ca puruṣasya kṛṣṇasya sāreṇa balena yo nipāta-vegas tena saṅkṣepābhitānām uragāṇāṁ viṣair unnato’mbu-rāśir yasya saḥ | viṣeṇa kaṣāyī-kṛtā rakta-pīta-varṇī-kṛtā bhayaṅkarā ūrmayo yasya saḥ | niryāse’pi kaṣāyo strī ity atra kṣīra-svāminā tathā vyākhyānāt | paryak paritaḥ dhanuḥ-śataṁ plutaḥ prasṛtaḥ—

aṣṭabhir yava-madhyaiḥ syād aṅgulaṁ tais tribhir bhavet |

tālaṁ tritālako hasto hastau dvau kiṣkur ucyate |

kiṣku-dvayaṁ dhanuḥ proktam || iti ||7||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : puruṣasya hareḥ sāreṇa balena yo nipāta-vegas tenāsaṅkṣobhitānām uragāṇāṁ viṣair ucchavasita unnato’mbur-āśir yasya saḥ | viṣe kaṣāyāṁ kvātharaso rakta-pīta-varṇatā tena vibhīṣaṇā bhayaṅkarā ūrmayo yasya sa sarpa-hṛdaḥ paryak parito dhanuḥ-śataṁ plutaḥ prasṛto’bhūt—

aṣṭabhir yavamadhyaiḥ syād aṅgulaṁ tair stribhir bhavet |

tālaṁ tri-tālako hastau hastau dvau kiṅ kurucyate ||

kiṣkudvayaṁ dhanuḥ proktam |

iti vacanāt dhanur mānaṁ bodhyam ||7||

...

|| 10.16.8 ||

tasya hrade viharato bhuja-daṇḍa-ghūrṇa-

vār-ghoṣam aṅga vara-vāraṇa-vikramasya |

āśrutya tat-sva-sadanābhibhavaṁ nirīkṣya

cakṣuḥ-śravāḥ samasarat tad amṛṣyamāṇaḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : hrade viharato bhuja-daṇḍāhatodaka-ghoṣaṁ śrutvā tataḥ sva-sadanābhibhavaṁ nirīkṣya tad asahamānaḥ sarpaḥ samasarat samājagāma ||8||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : aṅga he nṛpa | vara-vāraṇo garjendras tad vad vikramasya | yad vā, aṅgair avayavair varaḥ śreṣṭho yo vāraṇas tat-sama-vikramasya | tataḥ śrī-kṛṣṇāt | sva-sadanābhibhavaṁ svasyānaparābhvaṁ | samājagāma bādhitum ājagāmety arthaḥ | varaḥ śreṣṭheviṭapatau ca devāder īpsate vate iti yādavaḥ ||8||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : viharato vicitra-jala-vādya-santārādi-krīḍā kurvataḥ | aṅgety avyayam, rājñaḥ śoka-nirāsārthaṁ sa-lālana-sambodhane, he aṅga-svarūpātmīyeti vā | vara-vāraṇo matta-gajendras tadvad-vikramo jalāhati-krīḍā yasya | kiṁ vā, jale'pi vara-vāraṇavad vikramo līlā-gatir yasya | yad vā, aṅge kaśmiśṁcit śrī-pādābja-kaniṣṭhāṅgulya-vayave'pi vāra-vāraṇād airāvatād api vikramaḥ śakti-viśeṣo yasya tasyeti | bhuja-daṇḍābhyām āhatasya jalasya mahā-śabdo nādbhūta iti bhāvaḥ | nirikṣyālocya cakṣusaiva śṛṇotīti cakṣuḥ-śravā iti tad-ghoṣa-śravaṇe cakṣu-pravṛttes tasya vakṣyamāṇa-saundaryaṁ na kiñcid ākalayad iti bhāvaḥ, anyathā daṁśanāsambhavāt | yad vā, cakṣuṣi śravaḥ kīrtir yasya cakṣuṣā śravaṇasyāpi siddhes tat sāphalyaṁ tv adhunaivābhūd iti bhāvaḥ ||8||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : viharataḥ vicitra-jala-vādya-santārādi-krīḍāṁ kurvataḥ | vara-vāraṇo digdhastī tadvad vikramasya aṅtyevyayaṁ rājñaḥ śoka-nirāsārthaṁ salālana-sambodhanam ||8||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, jīva-gosvāmī bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : virahataḥ vicitra-jala-vādya-santārādinā krīḍataḥ bhuja-daṇḍābhyāṁ ghūrṇā yeṣāṁ tathā-bhūtānāṁ vārāṁ jalānāṁ ghoṣaṁ śrutvā tattato ghoṣād eva sva-sadanasyābhibhavaṁ nirīkṣya tattam asahamānaḥ ||8||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : tasya vihatato vicira-jala-vādya-santārādinā vikrīḍato bhuja-daṇḍābhyāṁ ghūrṇā yeṣāṁ tādṛśānāṁ vārāṁ ghoṣam āśrutya tad doṣād eva sva-sadanasyābhibhavaṁ ca nirīkṣya tat-tad amṛṣyamāṇo’sahaṁ cakṣuḥ-śravāḥ sarpaḥ samasarad āgataḥ ||8||

...

|| 10.16.9 ||

taṁ prekṣaṇīya-sukumāra-ghanāvadātaṁ

śrīvatsa-pīta-vasanaṁ smita-sundarāsyam |

krīḍantam apratibhayaṁ kamalodarāṅghriṁ

sandaśya marmasu ruṣā bhujayā cachāda ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : prekṣaṇīyaś ca sukumāraś ca ghana-vad ujjvalaś ca tam | śrīvatsa-pīta-vasanaṁ hrade viharataḥ śrīvatsena saṁyuktam uccalat pītaṁ vastraṁ yaya tam | bhujayā bhogena cacchāda aveṣṭayat ||9||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : taṁ kṛṣṇam avadātaḥ śyāmaḥ, avadāte'ruṇe pīte śvete śyāme nirāmaye iti śāśvataḥ | marmasu tāraṇādinātīva pīḍā mṛtyur vā jāyate, tat sthānaṁ marmeti | apratibhayaṁ nirbhayam | bhujayā bhogena śarīreṇa, bhogaḥ sukhe dhane cāheḥ śarīra-phaṇayor api iti medinī | bhujati kuṭilī-bhavatīti bhujā, bhujo kauṭilye ig-upadhatvāt kaḥ ||9||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : taṁ prekṣaṇīyety-ādi | prekṣaṇīyaḥ sundaraḥ sukumāraḥ sukomalo ghana iva ghana-śyāmala iti yāvat, tathāpy avadātam ujjvalaṁ śrīr lakṣmīs tad yuktaṁ vatsaṁ vakṣas tatra pīta-vasanaṁ yasya | tāvatā āvegenāpi jala-nipāte vakṣasaḥ pītavāso na skhalitam iti tātparyam ||9||

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tādṛśe'pi duṣṭo'sau tathāceṣṭateti kāliyasya mahāparādhaṁ darśayan bhāva-viśeṣeṇānuśocan viśinaṣṭi, taṁ prekṣaṇīyety-ādidinā | krīḍantam ity arthaḥ hetuḥ—apratibhayam iti | yad vā, apratibhayam api saṁdaśya cachādeti kāliyasya nirbuddhitvaṁ sūcayati | yad vā, apratibhayaṁ yathā syāt tathā sandaśya cachāda | cakṣuḥ-śravā iti prastutatvād eva jñeyam | kayā ? bhajayā bhogena, bhujagasya bhogenaiva gamanena bhujasyaiva bhogatvāt, "bhujagaḥ" iti pāṭhe'pi sa evārthaḥ | tattvatas tu marmasu daśanaṁ tat tad aṅga-cumbana-sauṣṭhavaṁ bhojenācchādanaṁ ca sarvāṅga-niviḍāliṅganam iti jñeyam, ato rupeva rūṣā vastutas tu premṇaiveti, yataḥ prekṣaṇīyety-ādi, ata evāgre taṁ prati śrī-bhagavato mahānugraho bhāvīti ||9||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tādṛśo’pi duṣṭo’sau tathā ceṣṭateti kāliyasya mahāparādhaṁ darśayan vraja-jana-bhāvenānuśocan viśinaṣṭi—prekṣaṇīyety-ādinā | krīḍantam ity atra hetuḥ apratibhayam iti tac ca kāliyasya nirbuddhitvaṁ sūcayati | cakṣuḥ-śravā iti, prastutatvād eva jñeyaṁ, kayā bhujayā bhujākāratvāt tasya bhoga eva bhujā yato bhujaga ity api ucyate | tasmād bhogenety arthaḥ | bhujaga iti pāṭhe bhogeneti śeṣaḥ ||9||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : śrī-yuktaṁ vatsaṁ vakṣo yasya pītaṁ vasanaṁ yasya sa, ca sa ca taṁ bhujākāratvāt tasya bhoga eva bhujā, yato bhujaga ity api ucyate, tasmād bhogenety arthaḥ | bhujaga iti pāṭhe bhogeneti śeṣaḥ ||9.13||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : atha kāliyasya daurjanya-varṇana-dvārā saubhāgya-viśeṣam eva varṇayann āha—taṁ prekṣaṇīyety-ādi | taṁ śrī-kṛṣṇaṁ bhujaś cachāda | dvitvābhāvas tv ārṣaḥ, bhogena chādayāmāsa, tad aṅga-saṅgāliṅganena saubhāgyam eva | kiṁ kṛtvā ? rūṣā marmasu sandaśya sac-cid-ānanda-ghaneṣu marmasu daśanair ālikhya | prekṣaṇīyo navoditatvād atiśaya-darśanīyaḥ sukumāraḥ susnigdho yo ghanas tat-svarūpo’pi avadātaḥ suṣṭhu-tejāḥ avadātaḥ site pītaḥ ity ukteḥ tejasaś ca śubhra-darmatvāt | varṇa-mahimā ghanābhas tejo-mahimnā sarva-prakāśa ity arthaḥ | yad vā, ghanāvadāto ghana-jyotir ghanānandavat śrī-vatse śrī-yukte lakṣmī-lakṣma-bhūṣite vatse vakṣati pīta-vasanaṁ yasya | yad vā, śrī-vatsaṁ dakṣiṇāvartaṁ sūkṣma-roma-rājiḥ, śrīr lakṣmīr vakṣaḥ-sthala-sthā lakṣmīs tasyā vatsam uraś ca | paścāt śrī-vatsaṁ ca śrī-vatsakṣety ekaśeṣe śrī-vatsam, tatra pīta-vasanaṁ prālambākāreṇa sthitaṁ pītottarīyaṁ yasya | etena tādṛśāsphalāna-vegenāpi yathā-sthitam uttarīyaṁ ca na lagitam ity avahelā niḥśaṅkatādikaṁ dhvanitam | ataḥ sthita-sundarāsyaṁ smita-yuktaṁ sundarāsyam, na tu smitena yati yati asau daśati, tati taty evāsya smitaṁ vardhate, tena yuktaṁ sundaraṁ mukhaṁ yasyety arthaḥ | apratibhayaṁ yathā-syāt-tathā krīḍantaṁ tena sahaiva krīḍantam iva, etena bhagavataḥ kopābhāvaḥ sūcitaḥ ||9-12||

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : prekṣaṇīyam atisukhadam api rūpaṁ kāliyaṁ prati viparītam abhūd ity āha—tam iti | ghanavad ujjvalaṁ śrī-vatse vihāra-vaśāt āyātaṁ pīta-vasanaṁ yasya tam | yad vā, śriyā lakṣmī-rekhayā yuktaṁ vatsaṁ vakṣo yasya pīte vasane yasya sa ca sa ca tam | uro vatsaṁ ca vakṣaś ca ity amaraḥ | bhujayā bhogena ||9||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : parama-manojñam api tad rūpaṁ kāliyaṁ prati viparītam abhūd ity āha—tam iti | ghanāvadātaṁ meghavad ujjvalaṁ śriyā rekhā-rūpayā yuktaṁ vatsam uro yasya pīte vasane yasya sa ca sa ca tam | bhujayā bhogena ||9||

...

|| 10.16.10 ||

taṁ nāga-bhoga-parivītam adṛṣṭa-ceṣṭam

ālokya tat-priya-sakhāḥ paśupā bhṛśārtāḥ |

kṛṣṇe’rpitātma-suhṛd-artha-kalatra-kāmā

duḥkhānuśoka-bhaya-mūḍha-dhiyo nipetuḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : sa priyo yeṣāṁ te tat-priyās te ca te sakhāyaś ceti tathā ||10.11||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : sa śrī-kṛṣṇaḥ | parivītaṁ pariveṣṭitam | na dṛṣṭā ceṣṭā iti hasta-pādādi-cālana-rūpā yasya tam ||10||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tasya priya-sakhā iti parama-sauhārdam uktam | paśupā iti svabhāvataḥ susnigdha-cittatvam | kṛṣṇe arpitā ātmanaḥ suhṛd-ādayo yair ātmanaḥ suhṛd-ādayaś ceti vā, tatra suhṛdaḥ pitṛ-bhrātr-ādayaḥ, arthā dhanāni, kāmā loka-dvaya-bhogāḥ | suhṛc-chabdena gṛhītasyāpi kalatrasya pṛthaṅ-nirdeśo'rpaṇa-viśeṣa-vivakṣayety ananyāpekṣatvam | ato bhṛśārtā atyarthaṁ duḥkhitāḥ santaḥ, ārta-svareṇa krandanto vā, ata eva duḥkhenānuśoko vāraṁ vāraṁ śocanaṁ bhayaṁ cānāthatvādinā, tābhyāṁ mūḍhā viveka-hīnā dhīr yeṣām, tathā-bhūtā naṣṭa-cetanā vā santaḥ |

yad vā, nāga-bhoga-parivītam ālokya ādau bhṛśārtāḥ, adṛṣṭa-ceṣṭaṁ cālokya duḥkhānuśoka-bhayair mūḍha-dhiyaḥ santo nitarāṁ chinna-mūla-vṛkṣavad aceṣṭatvādinā petuḥ | mūḍha-dhītvād eva te tad dhradaṁ na prāviśann iti jñeyam | adṛṣṭa-ceṣṭaṁ ca śrī-vṛndāvanāśritasyāpi kāliyasya niḥsāraṇāya teśv anyeṣu ca lokeṣu tad-doṣātiśaya-pradarśanārthaṁ tat premāliṅganānandena stabdhī-bhūtam iti tattvārthaḥ ||10||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tasya priya-sakhā iti parama-sauhārdam uktaṁ paśupā iti svabhāva-sāralyena susnigdha-cittatvaṁ kṛṣṇe arpitā ātmānaḥ suhṛd-ādayaś ca yais te, tat-sāhāyyāya kṛta-sarvārpaṇā ity arthaḥ | tatra suhṛdaḥ pitṛ-bhrātr-ādayaḥ, arthā dhanāni, kāmā loka-dvaya-bhogāḥ | suhṛc-chabdena gṛhītasyāpi kalatrasya pṛthaṅ nirdeśo viśeṣa-vivakṣayā, kintu kalatra-padena kecil labdha-yajñopavītā ye jyeṣṭhāḥ, te ca sakhāyo labhyanta iti sarveṣāṁ teṣām ananyāpekṣatvam | ato bhṛśārtā atyartha-duḥkhitāḥ santaḥ ārta-svareṇa krandanto vā | ata eva duḥkhena anuśokaḥ vāraṁ vāraṁ śocanaṁ bhayaṁ ca "taṁ vinā kathaṁ bhaviṣyāmaḥ ?" iti tābhyāṁ mūḍhā viveka-hīnā dhīr yeṣāṁ tathā-bhūtā naṣṭa-cetanā vā santaḥ |

yad vā, nāga-bhoga-parivītam ālokya ādau bhṛśārtāḥ adṛṣṭa-ceṣṭaṁ cālokya duḥkhānuśoka-bhayair mūḍha-dhiyaḥ santo nitarāṁ chinna-mūla-vṛkṣavad aceṣṭatvādinā petuḥ, mūḍha-dhītvād eva taṁ hradaṁ na prāviśann iti jñeyam | taj-jalāpluta-deśa-patane'py eṣāṁ viṣākrāntatvābhāvaḥ | śrī-kṛṣṇasya sparśa-prabhāvena hradasyāpi nirviṣīkaraṇāt adṛṣṭa-ceṣṭitatvaṁ ca kāliyasya niḥsāraṇāya tasmiṁs tat-patnīṣu ca tad-doṣātiśaya-pradarśanārtham ||10||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : parivītaṁ veṣṭitam adṛṣṭa-ceṣṭam iti | kāliyasyotsāha-vardhanārthaṁ kṣaṇaṁ bhītaṁ stabdhavat sthitam | yad vā, "are kāliya ! tvayā yatheṣṭaṁ prathamaṁ daśyatāṁ veṣṭyatām, ahaṁ paścād balaṁ darśayiṣyāmi" iti vīra-darpeṇa sthitam | paśupāḥ kecid gopāḥ śāli-kṣetra-sthāḥ kṛṣakāś ca śīghram āyātāḥ | te kīdṛśāḥ ? kṛṣṇe’rpitā lālanārtham ātmādayo yais te ||10||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : parivītaṁ veṣṭitaṁ adṛṣṭa-ceṣṭam iti "tvayā ādau yatheṣṭaṁ veṣṭyatām, ahaṁ tu paścād vikramaṁ darśayiṣyāmi" iti vīra-darpeṇa sthitam ity arthaḥ | paśupāḥ kecid gopāḥ śāli-kṣetra-sthāḥ karṣakāś ca śīghram āgatāḥ | te kīdṛśāḥ ? lālanārthaṁ kṛṣṇe’rpitātmādayo yais te | kāma-bhogyāḥ duḥkhānuśoko muhuḥ śocanaṁ "taṁ vinā kathaṁ bhaviṣyāmaḥ ?" iti bhayaṁ, tābhyāṁ mūḍhā nirvivekā dhīr yeṣāṁ tādṛśāḥ santo nipetuś chinna-mūla-taruvan nitarāṁ petuḥ ||10||

...

|| 10.16.11 ||

gāvo vṛṣā vatsataryaḥ krandamānāḥ suduḥkhitāḥ |

kṛṣṇe nyastekṣaṇā bhītā rudatya iva tasthire ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : vatsataryaḥ garbhādhāna-yogya-vayasaḥ ||11||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : krandamānā ārta-nādam uccaiḥ kurvatyaḥ | iva iti lokoktau | krandantyo rudatyo'śrūṇi muñcantyaḥ | tasthire iti parama-vatsalānāṁ gavādīnām atyanta-śokenātistabdhatāpatteḥ | kadācid vajra-viśeṣa-ghātena mṛtasyāpi prāṇina ūrdhvāvasthitivat | ātmanepadam ārṣam | vatsatarāṇāṁ vṛṣeṣv antarbhāvo jñeyaḥ, prādhānyād gavayo vyaktam uktāḥ | kiṁ ca, mahiṣy-ādayo grāhyā hariṇy-ādayaś ca | vanyāḥ sarve'pi paśavas tādṛg-avasthā eva jñeyāḥ, paśūṁś ca [10.16.19] iti vakṣyamāṇatvāt | teṣāṁ gavādibhyaḥ kiñcid-dūracaratvena paścād āgamanād atrānuktir iti jñeyam ||11||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : krandamānāṁ ārta-nādam uccaiḥ kurvatyaḥ | iva iti lokoktau | rudantyaḥ rudatyaḥ aśrūṇi muñcantyaḥ | tasthire iti parama-vatsalānāṁ gavādīnām atyanta-śokenāpi stabdhatāpatteḥ kadācid vajra-viśeṣa-ghātena mṛtasyāpi prāṇina ūrdhvāvasthitivat | ātmanepadam ārṣaṁ | gavādy-upalakṣitatvena mahiṣy-ādayo hariṇy-ādayaś ca jñeyāḥ, paśūṁś ca [10.16.19] iti vakṣyamāṇatvāt | teṣāṁ kiñcid dūra-caratvena paścād āgamanād atrānuktir iyam iti jñeyam ||11||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : rudantya iva iti bhaya-vaiyāgryeṇāśrūṇāṁ śoṣāt ||11||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : rudantya iva iti bhaya-vaiyāgryeṇa tad-aśrūṇāṁ viśoṣaṇāt ||11||

...

|| 10.16.12 ||

atha vraje mahotpātās trividhā hy atidāruṇāḥ |

utpetur bhuvi divy ātmany āsanna-bhaya-śaṁsinaḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : bhuvi bhū-kampādayaḥ, divi ulkā-pātādayaḥ, ātmani vāma-netra-sphuraṇādayaḥ | āsannaṁ bhayaṁ śaṁsituṁ śīlaṁ yeṣāṁ te ||12||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : āsannam nikaṭam ||12||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : atha anantaram eva atidāruṇāḥ svarūpato mahā-bhayaṅkarā mahotpātāś ca mahā-durnimitta-svabhāvataḥ | kiṁ vā, pūrvokta-nyāyena bhāgavata-pravara-śrī-nandādiṣu kāliyasya parama-duṣṭatā-pradarśanāya eteṣāṁ tatrāgamanārtham ||12||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : atha anantaram eva dāruṇāḥ svabhāvato mahā-bhayaṅkarāḥ mahotpātāś ca mahā-durnimittaṁ svabhāvataḥ ||12||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : trividhāḥ--bhuvi bhū-kampādayaḥ, divi ulkā-pātādayaḥ, ātmani vāma-netra-sphuraṇādayaḥ | bhagavataḥ khalv amaṅgalā śaṅkā-rāhitye’pi yad utpāta-prākaṭyaṁ tad gavāṁ gopādīnāṁ ca duḥkha-sūcanārthaṁ | kiṁ vā, tat-tad-adhiṣṭhātṛ-devānām api kṛṣṇe prītimattvenaiśvarya-vismaraṇāt | kṛṣṇe’py aśubhāśaṅkina utpātaṁ prakaṭayāmaṣur iti ||12||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : trividhāḥ--bhuvi bhū-kampādayaḥ, divy ulkā-pātādayaḥ, ātmani ca vāma-netra-spandanādayaḥ | iyam aśubha-sūcanā tal-līlā-śaktyaiva tatra vrajaukasāṁ vikṣubdha-manaskatayā samākarṣaṇāya racitā | nahi bhagavati saparikare sā santaret ||12||

...

|| 10.16.13-15 ||

tān ālakṣya bhayodvignā gopā nanda-purogamāḥ |

vinā rāmeṇa gāḥ kṛṣṇaṁ jñātvā cārayituṁ gatam ||

tair durnimittair nidhanaṁ matvā prāptam atad-vidaḥ |

tat-prāṇās tan-manaskās te duḥkha-śoka-bhayāturāḥ ||

ābāla-vṛddha-vanitāḥ sarve’ṅga paśu-vṛttayaḥ |

nirjagmur gokulād dīnāḥ kṛṣṇa-darśana-lālasāḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : tān ālakṣya gokulān nirjagmur iti tṛtīyenānvayaḥ | bhayodvignā bhaya-hetubhyo bhītāḥ | bhayena kampamānā iti vā ||13|| na taṁ vidantīty atad-vidaḥ ||14|| paśu-vṛttayo ’tivatsalāḥ ||15||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : tān utpātān | bhaya-hetubhyo mahotpātebhyaḥ | lakṣaṇāto varā śaktiḥ ity uktim āśritya samāsāntaram āha—bhayena iti | udvignā ity ovijī bhaya calanayoḥ | ataḥ kta-pratyayaḥ, tasya natvaṁ ca ||13|| tair bhū-kampādibhiḥ | atad-vidaḥ kṛṣṇa-māhātmyam ajānantaḥ | tasmin kṛṣṇe indriyāṇi yeṣāṁ te tathā | nitarāṁ dhanaṁ śrī-yamunā-hrada-rūpaṁ sva-vihārāspadam iti sarasvatī-samvādaḥ ||14|| bālān abhivyāpyābālaṁ, tat-prabhṛti ye vṛddhā vanitāḥ te ābāla-vṛddha-vanitāḥ | paśupa-dṛṣṭi-mātrāt yad vyavahāro yeṣāṁ, te tathā ||15||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : nidhanaṁ matvā iti | rāmeṇa vinā gāś cārayituṁ gataṁ kṛṣṇaṁ jñātvā sveṣāṁ maraṇaṁ prāptaṁ samāgatam iti matvā ca ||14||

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : vinā rāmeṇa iti parama-snigdhasya tasya sākṣād anujāmaṅgalaṁ na sambhaved iti bhāvaḥ | anyait tair vyākhyātam | yad vā, ālakṣya eva bhayodvignā babhūvuḥ, ata eva nidhanaṁ prāptaṁ matvā eva atad-vidas tan-māhātmyam ananusandadhānā ity arthaḥ, yataḥ sa ca prāṇo jīvanaṁ yeṣām, atas tasminn eva mano yeṣām iti sarvathā tad-eka-gataya ity arthaḥ | te tad eka-priyatvena prasiddhāḥ, sva-pakṣe te gopāḥ, ato duḥkhādibhir āturā vivaśāḥ ||13.14|| ata eva dīnāḥ, itas tato muhuḥ skhalanto nipatantaś cety arthaḥ | kṛṣṇo vraja-jana-cittākarṣakaḥ, "kathaṁ kutrāsti ?" iti tad-darśanotsukāḥ santaḥ | yad vā, sadā svabhāvata eva kṛṣṇa-darśana-lālasā iti tad-eka-gatitvam eva darśitam ||15||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tān iti trikam | vinā rāmeṇa iti parama-snigdhasya gambhīrasya sadā sāhāyya-ratasya tasya samakṣe tasya tādṛśa-vyasanatā na sambhaved iti bhāvaḥ | anyat taiḥ | ālakṣyaiva bhayodvignā babhūvuḥ ||13|| ata eva nidhanam eva prāptaṁ matvā nitarāṁ dhanaṁ śrī-hrada-rūpaṁ sva-vihāra-sarvasvam iti sarasvatī-samvādaḥ | atad-vido māhātmyam ananusandadhānā ity arthaḥ | nanu, kathaṁ tatraiva teṣāṁ kṣobhaḥ jātaḥ ? tatrāha—sa eva prāṇo jīvanaṁ yeṣām iti ||14|| te tad-eka-priyatvena prasiddhāḥ paśu-vṛttitvena tadīya-vātsalya-svabhāva-bhāvitā ity arthaḥ | paśuvad-vṛtti vātsalyāṁśe yeṣām iti vā | ata eva dīnāḥ itas tato muhuḥ skhalanto nipatantaś cety arthaḥ | kṛṣṇaḥ vraja-jana-cittākarṣakaḥ "kathaṁ kutrāsti" iti tad-darśanotsukāḥ santaḥ | yad vā, svabhāvata eva tādṛśaḥ ||15||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : nidhanam iti nitarāṁ dhanaṁ śrī-yamunā-hrada-rūpam iti vāstavārthaḥ ||14.15||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : atha durnimittāni dṛṣṭvā gokula-vāsinām āśaṅkām āha—tān ālokya ity-ādi dvābhyām | tais tathā-vidhair durnimittair nidhanaṁ prāptaṁ samupasthitam iti matvā "aho’smākaṁ maraṇam upasthitam iva" iti matvā vinā rāmena gāś cārayituṁ gataṁ kṛṣṇaṁ ca jñātvā, "kim idam ākasmikaṁ kim abhivyañjakaṁ durnimittam ?" ity atad-vido durnimitta-kāraṇāvidaḥ sarve gokulān nirjagmur ity uttareṇānvayaḥ |

yad vā, rāmena vinā atad-vidaḥ | rāmas tu tadvad eva | ayaṁ vākyārthaḥ | etad durnimittaṁ yad dṛśyate, tad avagataṁ nidhanaṁ prāptam, maraṇa-sūcakāny evaitāni durnimittāni | "kṛṣṇo’pi na nikaṭa-vartī, yo’smān rakṣiṣyati | sa tu gāś cārayituṁ gataḥ, tadā tatraiva sarve gacchāmaḥ" iti sarve tathā cakruḥ | vastutas tu durnimittasya kāraṇaṁ kim api nāsti, bhagavataḥ sakala-vighnopaśama-hetutvād vighnasya śaṅkāpi nāsti | asati vipatti-sadbhāve kathaṁ tasya sūcakaṁ durnimittam ? tena etādṛśaṁ yad vīrya-kauśalaṁ sarvair dṛśyatām iti sarveṣāṁ tatrānayanārthaṁ bhagavataiva tat sampāditaṁ kuhakam, na tu tad vāstavam iti | ata evoktam āsanna-bhaya-śaṁsinaḥ [10.16.12] iti | atreyaṁ vyākhyātā—ete utpātā bhaya-śaṁsino nāsan bhagavantaṁ pratīti paramārthoktiḥ ||13.19||

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : tān ālakṣya gokulān nirjagmur iti tṛtīyenānvayaḥ | nidhanam eva prāptaṁ matvā nitarāṁ dhanaṁ śrī-yamunā-hrada-rūpaṁ sva-vihārāspādam iti sarasvatī-saṁvādaḥ, mahā-śokāt paśūnām iva buddhi-viveka-pratīkāra-jñāna-śūnyā vṛttiḥ sattā yeṣāṁ te ||13||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : tān ālakṣya gokulāt sarve nirjagmur iti tṛtīya-gatena sambandhaḥ rāmeṇa vinā gāś cārayituṁ gataṁ kṛṣṇaṁ jñātvā ||13|| nidhanaṁ nāśaṁ prāptaṁ matvā nitarāṁ dhanaṁ sva-vihāraṣpada-kālindyā hradam iti gīrdevyā sūcito’rthaḥ | atad-vidas tal-līlā-śakti-ceṣṭām ajānantaḥ ||14|| mahā-śokāt paśūnām iva tat-pratīkāra-bodha-hīnā vṛttir yeṣāṁ te ||15||

...

|| 10.16.16 ||

tāṁs tathā kātarān vīkṣya bhagavān mādhavo balaḥ |

prahasya kiñcin novāca prabhāvajño’nujasya saḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : kātarān bhītān ||16||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : tān bālādīnāṁ | tathā rodanādi-prakāreṇa | mādhavo madhu-vaṁśodbhūtaḥ | sa balaḥ | anujasya kṛṣṇasya prabhāvajñaḥ "ayaṁ tu sāksāt sarva-sarpeśvara-śeṣa-śāyī katham enam anyaḥ sarpaḥ pīḍayituṁ kalpaḥ ?" iti jñānavān ity arthaḥ | bhagavān sarva-śakti-yukto’pi mādhavaḥ sarva-vidyā-patir apy asamartha iva na kiñcid upadiṣṭavān kintu tad-duḥkhena duḥkhe’pi teṣām eva kiñcid vaiyārthyam prakaṭaṁ bahir evaṁ hiṁsitvā tūṣṇīm āsīt | ayaṁ nijānujasya tattvajñaḥ snigdhaś ca hasatīti nātra cinteti bodhayitum ity arthaḥ ||16|| [atra viśvanāthaḥ]

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tathā tādṛśān sarva-jñānādi-sampannān apīty arthaḥ | kātarān bhītā vihvalān vā | yad vā, tādṛśa-kārtarya-prāptān api tān vīkṣya sākṣād dṛṣṭvā so’nujaika-priyo’pi balaḥ, prahasya uccair hasitvā kiñcid api novāca, kiñcit prahasyeti vā, yato bhagavān sarvajñaḥ, viśeṣato mādhavo madhu-vaṁśodbhavaḥ, ata eva anujasya prabhāvam aiśvaryam | kiṁ vā, prakṛṣṭaṁ bhāvaṁ kāliya-niḥsāraṇārthaṁ tat-tad-ācaraṇābhiprāyaṁ jānātīti tathā saḥ | prahāsas teṣāṁ śokādi-lāghavārtham, kiñcid anuktiś ca śrī-bhagavad-abhiprāyābhijñatā-sūcanena teṣām āśvāsanāya | yad vā, prahāsena kiñcid anuktyā ca taṁ prati śrī-bādarāyaṇeḥ praṇaya-kopoktir iyam | bhagavān parama-svatantro nirapekṣo vā | mādhavo madhu-kula-sambandha-sphūrtyā, na gopavad atisnehārdra-citta ity arthaḥ | kiṁ vā, madhu-pāna-rasika ity arthaḥ | balo balavān eva, na tu buddhimān ity arthaḥ, yataḥ sa haladhara ity artha iti tasya tad yuktam eveti bhāvaḥ ||16||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tathā tādṛśa-kātaryaṁ prāptān api tān vīkṣya sa gokulaika-priyo’pi balaḥ bhagavān sarva-śakti-yukto’pi mādhavaḥ sarva-vidyāpatir apy asamartha iva kiñcin na kṛtavān ajña iva ca na kiñcid upadiṣṭavān, kintu tad-duḥkhena duḥkhito’pi teṣām eva kiñcit dveṣyārtham | pra-iti prakaṭaṁ bahir eva hasitvā tūṣṇīm āsīt | ayaṁ nijānujasya tattvajñaḥ snigdhasya hasatīti "nātra cintā" iti bodhayitum ity arthaḥ | evaṁ teṣāṁ prāṇa-rakṣā-yogyatvāt nāśa eva tadā tasyāvirbhūtaḥ svabhāvata eva sarva-samādhāna-śaktimayatvāt bhagaval-līlayā iti bhāvaḥ | tarhi katham īdṛśe’pi duḥkha-saṅkaṭe sva-sāmarthyaṁ na vyañjitavān | na ca tat-prabhāvaṁ spaṣṭam upadiṣṭavān ? tatrāha—prabhāvajña iti | taj-jñatvena tad-icchāṁ vinā tat kartuṁ na śaktavān ity arthaḥ | mādhava-padaṁ cedaṁ śrī-harivaṁśe vyutpāditam—

mā vidyā ca yataḥ proktā tasyā īśo yato bhavet |

tasmāt mādhava-nāmāsi dhavaḥ svāmīti kīrtitaḥ || iti ||16||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : bhagavān sarva-śakti-yukto’pi mādhavaḥ sarva-vidyā-patir ity asamartha iva na kiñcid upadiṣṭavān kintu tad-duḥkhena duḥkhe’pi teṣām eva kiñcid dhairyārthaṁ prakaṭaṁ bahir eva hasitvā tūṣṇīm āsīt | ayaṁ nijānujasya tattvajñaḥ snigdhaś ca hasatīti nātra cinteti bodhayitum ity arthaḥ | tasmāc ca hetor atiduḥkhitam ājñāya vicārya tarhi katham īdṛśe’pi saṅkaṭe sva-sāmarthyaṁ na vyañjitavān ? na ca tat-prabhāvaṁ spaṣṭam upadiṣṭavān, tatrāha—prabhāvajña iti | taj-jñatvena tad-icchāṁ vinā tat kartuṁ na śaktavān ity arthaḥ | mādhava-padaṁ cedaṁ śrī-hari-vaṁśe vyutpāditam—

mā vidyā ca yataḥ proktā tasyā īśo yato bhavet |

tasmān mādhava-nāmāsi dhavaḥ svāmīti kīrtitaḥ || iti ||16.20||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) :

mā vidyā ca yataḥ proktā tasyā īśo yato bhavet |

tasmān mādhava-nāmāsi dhavaḥ svāmīti kīrtitaḥ || iti hari-vaṁśokta-nirukteḥ |

prabhāvaṁ līlaiśvaryaṁ jānātīti saḥ, tasya svānuja-mahā-premavattve’pi premnā tad-aiśvaryānām āvaraṇaṁ kṛṣṇecchānurañjita-līlā-śaktaiva, anyathā śrī-nandādīn śokāvegena sarpa-hradaṁ maṅkṣu śīghraṁ mimaṅkṣūn ko vārayituṁ prabhavet ? iti bhāvaḥ | prahasya iti mat-svarūpeṇa śeṣa-nāgena saha krīḍā na rocate, kintu prākṛta-kṣudra-kāliya-sarpādhamenaiveti tasya nara-līlatva-smaraṇāt | kiñcin novāca iti teṣāṁ śokāndhānāṁ kṛṣṇaṁ didṛkṣūṇāṁ tad-āvaraṇasyānaucityād aśakyatvāc ca kintu sva-prahāsa-śokābhāva-darśanaṁ yato na kiñcid aniṣṭābhāvam ūhayitvā prāṇa-jihāsāṁ śithilayāmāsa ||16||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : prahasya iti | mad-avatāreṇa śeṣa-nāgena svaikāntinā sārdhaṁ krīḍā na rocate, kintv anena vimukhena nāgādhameneti prahāsaḥ | yadyapi nandādīnām iva baladevasyāpi kṛṣṇānurāgitvāt tad-aiśvarya-jñānācchādanena bhāvyaṁ, tathāpi teṣāṁ hrada-praveśa-pratiṣedhāya tad-icchānurañjitayā tal-līlā-śaktyā taj-jñānaṁ tasya nācchāditaṁ tat-prahāsānumita-tan-maṅgalānāṁ teṣāṁ loke vraja-bhavanaṁ loka-jihāsāpi nābhūd iti ||16||

...

|| 10.16.17 ||

te’nveṣamāṇā dayitaṁ kṛṣṇaṁ sūcitayā padaiḥ |

bhagaval-lakṣaṇair jagmuḥ padavyā yamunā-taṭam ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : bhagavantaṁ lakṣayanti yāni padāni, taiḥ padaiḥ sūcitayā padavya-mārgeṇa ||17||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : lakṣayanti sūcayanti padma-dhvajādi-cihnaiḥ ||17||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : dayitam iti tat-pada-lakṣaṇābhijñatvaṁ sadyas tat-prāptau hetuṁ ca dyotayati | dayitve hetuḥ—kṛṣṇam iti | ata eva anviṣyanto mṛgayamāṇāḥ ||17||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : anveṣamāṇāḥ anvicchantyaḥ | tatra hetuḥ--dayitaṁ | tatra hetuḥ--kṛṣṇam ||17||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : bhagavantaṁ lakṣayanti yāni, taiḥ padaiḥ sūcitayā padavyā ||17||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : bhagavantaṁ kṛṣṇaṁ lakṣayanti yāni, taiḥ padair añja-yavāṅkuśādi-cihnitaiḥ sūcitayā padavyā jagmuḥ ||17||

...

|| 10.16.18 ||

te tatra tatrābja-yavāṅkuśāśani-

dhvajopapannāni padāni viśpateḥ |

mārge gavām anya-padāntarāntare

nirīkṣamāṇā yayur aṅga satvarāḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : padair mārga-jñāna-prakāram āha—ta iti | viśpateḥ śrī-kṛṣṇasya anyeṣāṁ padānām antarāntare madhye madhye tat-tad-apohena | gavāṁ śrutīnāṁ mārge satvarāḥ apramattā yoginas tat-tad-upādhy-apavādena yathā paraṁ tattvaṁ mṛgayante tadvad iti bhāvaḥ ||18||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : te gopāḥ | viśāṁ prajānāṁ nandasya vaiśya-jātīyatvād vaiśyānāṁ vā pateḥ vayaṁ go-vṛttayo’niśam [bhā.pu. 10.24.21] ity ukteḥ | tatra tatra sthāne sthāne | aśanir vajram | anyeṣāṁ gavādīnāṁ | antaraś cāntaraś cānayoḥ samāhāro'ntarāntaram, tasmiṁs tathā madhye-madhye ity arthaḥ | tat-tad-apohena teṣāṁ teṣāṁ go-pādānāṁ gopa-padānāṁ cāpohena tyāgena | go-śabdasya śruti-vācakatvaṁ vāg vācitvād dhyeyam | śrutīnāṁ vedānām | mārge vicāra-rūpe | apramattāḥ sāvadhānāḥ | tat-tad-upādhy-apavādena annamayādy-upādhi-nirākaraṇena | paraṁ tattvaṁ śuddhaṁ brahma brahma pucchaṁ pratiṣṭhā [tai.u. 2.7.1] ity evaṁ puccha-rūpatvenānveṣayantīti | tadvad ity atra gavāṁ dhenūnāṁ mārge’dhvany api tathaivānveṣayāmāsuḥ | iti bhāva iti | saṅkalitāntaraṁ saṅkalitākaraṇaṁ vinā na labhyata iti tātparyam ||18||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : tatra tatra sarvatraiva śrīmat-pādābja-nyāsa-sthāne | nanu, ciraṁ prasthitasya paśupa-varga-pariveṣṭitasya tasya padāni na kathaṁ mṛṣṭāni ? kathaṁ vā paśu-pakṣy-ādibhir nākrāntāni ? tatrāha—viśāṁ prāṇa-dhanānāṁ patyur bhūmy-ātma-bhūṣaṇatvena prayatnato dhṛtāni vraja-vana-vāsināṁ mahā-dhanāni, na kenāpi tāny ākramyanta iti bhāvaḥ ||18||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : padaiḥ pada-vījñāna-prakāram āha—te iti | viśpateḥ viśāṁ vaiśyānāṁ gopānāṁ patyur adhyakṣasya kṛṣṇasya | yattvābhāva āryaḥ | anyeṣāṁ padānām antarāntare madhye madhye tat-tad-apohena gavāṁ śrutīnāṁ mārge satvarā apramattā yoginas tat-tad-upādhy-apavādena yathā paraṁ tattvaṁ mṛgayanti, tadvad iti bhāvaḥ ||18||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : padaiḥ pada-vījñāna-prakāram āha—te iti | viśpateḥ viśāṁ vaiśyānāṁ gopānāṁ patyur adhyakṣasya kṛṣṇasya | yattvābhāva āryaḥ | anyeṣāṁ padānām antarāntare madhye madhye tat-tad-apohena gavāṁ śrutīnāṁ mārge satvarā apramattā yoginas tat-tad-upādhy-apavādena yathā paraṁ tattvaṁ mṛgayanti, tadvad iti bhāvaḥ ||18||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : tad evāha—te tatreti | viśpateḥ viśāṁ gopānāṁ patyuḥ svāminaḥ anyeṣāṁ padānām antarāntare madhye madhye tat-tad apavādena tāni nirīkṣamāṇāḥ satvarā yayuḥ gavāṁ śrutīnāṁ mārge satvarāḥ yogino atan nirasanena yathā tam eva paraṁ tattvam anviṣyanti tadvad iti bhāvaḥ ||18||

...

|| 10.16.19 ||

antar hrade bhujaga-bhoga-parītam ārāt

kṛṣṇaṁ nirīham upalabhya jalāśayānte |

gopāṁś ca mūḍha-dhiṣaṇān paritaḥ paśūṁś ca

saṅkrandataḥ parama-kaśmalam āpur ārtāḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : tataś ca sarpa-śarīra-veṣṭitaṁ kṛṣṇaṁ dūrān nirīkṣya, gopāṁś ca paśūṁś ca tathā nirīkṣya, ārtāḥ parama-kaśmalaṁ paraṁ mohaṁ prāpuḥ ||19||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : tataś ca anantaraṁ ca | tathā tathā mūrchayā patitān saṅkrandataś ca | nirīham niśceṣṭam | antar hrade hrada-madhye | jalāśayānte hrada-samīpe | "bho bālakā vṛttāntaṁ tāvat kathayata—kiṁ kāliyenaiva tīrād ākṛṣya kṛṣṇo jale pātitaḥ ? kiṁ vā, kṛṣṇa eva tīrād avalplutya jale patitaḥ ? tatrāpi sva-buddhyā anyasya kasyacid upadeśena vā ?" ity-ādi-praśne mūḍha-dhiṣanān kim api vaktum asamarthān gopān vīkṣya parama-kaśmalaṁ tan mūrchāto’pi sakāśād adhikāṁ mūrcchām āpur ity arthaḥ ||19||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : mūḍha-dhiṣaṇān mohaṁ gatān, paritaḥ kiñcid adhikaika-yojana-vyāpi-tad-dhrada-pūrva-dakṣiṇa-paścima-prānteṣu samyak krandataḥ paśūṁś ca upalabhya sarvam eva hradaṁ vyāpya vikrīḍato bhagavate darśanārtham anantānāṁ paśūnāṁ tatra tatra sarvatraiva sa-saṅkrandanāv asthiteḥ | ca-kārād ubhayeṣām api tatra prādhānyaṁ bodhayataḥ | yad vā, eka ukta-samuccaye, tataś ca pakṣiṇo’pīti jñeyam ||19||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : mūḍha-dhiṣaṇāṁ mohaṁ gatān paritaḥ sarvaṁ eva tīraṁ vyapya ||19||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : sāmānyato gopa-gopī-janānāṁ vaiklavyam āha—tataś ca antar-hrade hrada-madhye bhujaga-bhoga-parītaṁ sarpa-śarīra-veṣṭitaṁ, "bho bālakāḥ, vṛttāntaṁ tāvat kathayata, kiṁ kāliyenaiva tīrāt kṛṣṇa-balād ākṛṣya jale pātitaḥ ? kiṁ vā, kṛṣṇa eva tīrād avaplutya jale patitaḥ ? tatrāpi sva-buddhyā anyasya kasyacid ādeśena vā ?" ity-ādi-praśne mūḍha-dhiyaḥ mūrcchita-buddhīn vaktuṁ kim api ceṣṭitaṁ cāsamarthān gopān vīkṣya parama-kaśmalaṁ tan-mūrcchātaḥ sakāśād apy atimūrcchām ||19||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : tatra yātās te kṛṣṇaṁ nirīhaṁ gopān mūḍha-dhiṣaṇān paśūṁś ca saṅkrandata ārād dūrād upalabhya dṛṣṭvā paramaṁ kaśmalam āpuḥ ||19||

...

|| 10.16.20 ||

gopyo’nurakta-manaso bhagavaty anante

tat-sauhṛda-smita-viloka-giraḥ smarantyaḥ |

graste’hinā priyatame bhṛśa-duḥkha-taptāḥ

śūnyaṁ priya-vyatihṛtaṁ dadṛśus trilokam ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : priyeṇa śrī-kṛṣṇena vyatihṛtaṁ virahitaṁ trailokyaṁ śūnyaṁ dadṛśuḥ ||20.21||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : śūnyam asat-tulyam, śūnyam aṅkāsane khe ca śva-samūhe vitathe tathā iti dharaṇiḥ ||20||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : gopyo'nurakta-manasa ity-ādi | gopyo'tra yaśodā-sahacaryaḥ, tulya-vyathāḥ [bhā.pu. 10.16.21] iti viśeṣaṇāt, tāsāṁ taṁ prati putra-bhāva eva, bhogyatvenānuraktās tu navīnāḥ, tāsāṁ duḥkhaṁ tu rāga-viṣayam | tena tan noddiṣṭam, tās tu mūrcchayaiva datta-hastāvalambās tāvantaṁ kālam āsan, bhagavac-chaktyaiva punar jīvitā iti bhāvaḥ ||20||

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : evaṁ sarveṣām eva sāmānyena duravasthām uktvā tatraiva śrī-gopīnāṁ viśeṣam āha—gopya iti | bhagavati sarvaiśvarya-yukte’to na vidyate anto nāśo bhaktānām api yasmāt, tathā-bhūte’paricchinna iti vā, ity ahi-grasanāsambhava uktaḥ, tathāpy ahinā graste tad-icchayaiva bhogenākrānte atyartha-duḥkha-taptāḥ, yato’nurakta-manasaḥ svabhāvato nirantara-premavatya ity arthaḥ | tathā tasmāt tasminn api svabhāvataḥ priyatama eveti ātmā priyaḥ paramātmā priyataraḥ, tato’pi viśiṣṭatvāt śrī-kṛṣṇas priyatamaḥ, eveti tataḥ prema-bharākrāntyā tat tattvān anusandhānād ity arthaḥ |

yad va, bhagavati parama-sundare’nante cāparicchinna-guṇe tathā priyatame svabhāvata eva sarvato’dhika-priye, ataḥ sadā anurakta-manasa eva | adhunā ca graste grastavat sarvato bhogena pariveṣṭīte sati tasya sauhṛdena premṇā yāḥ smitāvaloka-giras tāḥ smarantyo bhṛśa-duḥkha-taptāḥ satyaḥ | śūnyam iti śloka-vegena ātmana iva jagatām api maraṇa-mananān nija-priyatamābhāvena sarvasyaivābhāva-mananād vā ||20||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : evaṁ sarveṣām eva sāmānyena duravasthām uktvā tatraiva śrī-gopīnāṁ viśeṣam āha—gopya iti | bhagavati sarvaiśvarya-yukte ato na vidyate’nto nāśo bhaktānāṁ yasmāt, tathā-bhūte aparicchinna iti vā ity ahi-grasanāsambhava uktaḥ | tathāpy ahinā graste tad-icchyaiva bhogenākrānte sati atyartha-duḥkha-taptāḥ, yato’nurakta-manasaḥ svabhāvato nirantara-premavatya ity arthaḥ | tathā tasmāt tasminn api svabhāvataḥ priyatama eveti ātmā priyaḥ, paramātmā priyataraḥ, tato’pi viśiṣṭatvāt śrī-kṛṣṇaḥ priyatama eveti | tataḥ prema-bharākrāntyā tat tattvān anusandhānād ity arthaḥ |

yad vā, bhagavati parama-sundare anante ca aparicchinna-guṇe, tathā priyatame kasyacit priyatare, tāsāṁ tu priyatame, yataḥ sadā anurakta-manasaḥ | adhunā ca graste grastavat sarvato bhogena pariveṣṭite sati, tasya sauhṛdena premṇā yāḥ smitāvaloka-giraḥ, tāḥ smarantyo bhṛśa-duḥkha-taptāḥ satyaḥ priyeṇaiva kartrā viśeṣeṇa atiśayena hṛtaṁ sva-grastatā-darśanāya andha-vadhirāyamāṇatāṁ vidhāya ca vismāritam ity arthaḥ | "priya-vyatikṛtam" iti pāṭhe ca, vyatikaraḥ samākhyāto vyasane vyatiṣañjane iti viśva-prakāśāt, vyasanaṁ vipadi bhraṁśe ity amara-kośāc ca | priyeṇa hetunā bhraṣṭam iti | tathaivārthaḥ ṭīkāyāṁ virahitam ity asya ca tyājitam ity arthaḥ | iti tathaiva tātparyaṁ tatas tādṛśaṁ jagat-chūnyaṁ śūnyam iti śoka-vegenātmana iva jagatām iti maraṇa-mananāt nija-priyatamābhāvena sarvasyaikārakāra-gamanād vā ||20||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : bhagavato līlā-viśeṣa evāyam | māṁ prati kasya kīdṛk prema ? iti parīkṣitaṁ samāgateṣv api sarveṣu teṣu kṣaṇam ahi-bhoga-baddhaḥ san kautukam akārṣīt | tatra tasya prabhāva-jñānām api gopa-gopīnāṁ pitroś ca aniṣṭa-śaṅkīni bandhu-hṛdayāni iti nyāyena mahijñatām api bādhitvāprema-prabhāveṇa sarveṣāṁ karuṇa eva raso jātaḥ | prema-vātsalye tasya prema-vardhakatvenāṅgatvam evāpatur ity āha—gopyo’nurakta-manasa ity-ādi dvābhyām |

gopyo’tra vrajeśvarī-tulya-vayasaḥ, tulya-vyathā [bhā.pu. 10.16.21] iti | vātsalya-pratipādakatvād anurakta-manaso viklinna-hṛdayā bhagavaty anante’hinā graste ruddhe sati śunyaṁ jagad dadṛśuḥ | priya-vyatikṛtaṁ priyam abhīṣṭaṁ tena virahitaṁ tu kevalaṁ śūnyaṁ dadṛśuḥ | tāḥ kṛṣṇa-mātaraṁ vrajeśvarīm apatyaṁ śrī-kṛṣṇaṁ prati anuprataprām, tulya-vyathāḥ satyaḥ samupaguhya śucaḥ sravantyas tās tāḥ kathāḥ kathayantya āsann ity uttareṇānvayaḥ | etena tulya-vyathatvāt tat-savayasa eva | yās tu bhagavat-priyāḥ kiśoryaḥ, tāsāṁ duḥkhānubhavo na vāg-viṣaya eveti tan noktam, tad-avarṇanam eva varṇanam, tās tu mūrchayaiva dattāś cāsās tad-duḥkha-jñās tathā na babhūvuḥ | paścāt taṭam utthite bhagavati yadā harṣa-jaya-kolāhalo’bhūt, tadaiva tāḥ prabodhaṁ gatā iti tad-duḥkhāvarṇanāt tathaiva mantavyam ||20.23||

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : tatrānurāgavatīnāṁ vaiklavyam āha—bhagavati parama-sundare ananta-guṇe tasya sauhṛdaṁ sva-viṣayakaṁ prema-smitaṁ vilokaṁ rahasi kṛtāṁ giraṁ saurata-vārtāṁ ca smarantyaḥ trilokaṁ priyeṇa vyatikṛtaṁ virahitaṁ tad-viraha-dāvāgni-bhasmībhūtatvāc chūnyam | “vyatihṛtam” iti pāṭhe priyeṇaiva viśeṣeṇa atiśayena ca hṛtaṁ sva-daśāntaḥ-pātīti kṛtaṁ dadṛśuḥ ||20||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : vraja-kiśorīṇām avasthām āha—gopya iti | bhagavati parama-sundare anante bṛhad-guṇatvād yam antam āhuḥ ity ukter mahā-guṇa-śālini kṛṣṇe’nurakta-manasaḥ, tasya sauhṛdaṁ sva-viṣayāṁ maitrīṁ smita-vilokaṁ giraṁ ca raho vārtāṁ smarantyaḥ trilokaṁ priyeṇa tena vyatihṛtaṁ virahitaṁ tad-viraha-vahni-dagdhatvāc chūnyaṁ dadṛśuḥ ||20||

...

|| 10.16.21 ||

tāḥ kṛṣṇa-mātaram apatyam anu prataptāṁ

tulya-vyathāḥ samanugṛhya śucaḥ sravantyaḥ |

tās tā vraja-priya-kathāḥ kathayantya āsan

kṛṣṇānane'rpita-dṛśo mṛtaka-pratīkāḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : tā gopyaḥ | kṛṣṇa-mātaraṁ yaśodām | sūco’śrūṇi | sravantyo’śru-dhārayā nadīṁ kurvatyaḥ | vraja-priyasya kṛṣṇasya kathās tās tāḥ pūtanā-vadhādi-rūpāḥ | vā vrajasya priya-kathāḥ—"pūtanādyā mahā-duṣṭā anena bahavo hatāḥ | ayaṁ sarpas teṣv ekaḥ, ko nāma varāka enaṁ hatvādhunaivāyasyati ?" iti tan-mātrāśvāsanārtham iti jīva-gosvāminaḥ | pratīko ’paghane tulye iti dharaṇiḥ | mṛta-tulyā iti ||21||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : nanu, tan-mātā kā hanta kīdṛśī jātā ? ity apekṣāyāṁ śoka-bhareṇa tat kiñcid eva prakāśayan sarvāsām eva tāsāṁ daśā-viśeṣam āha—tā iti, na patati kasminn api duḥkhe kulaṁ yasmāt, tad apatyaṁ parama-sneha-pātra-putram ity arthaḥ | ata eva tad anu lakṣīkṛtya tad-arthaṁ prakarṣeṇa sarvato’dhikatayā taptām | tulya-vyathā api samyak anu nirantaraṁ gṛhītvā dhṛtvā, anyathā tasyāḥ sahasaiva sadyo-hrada-praveśāpatteḥ | śucaḥ ślokāśrūṇi sravantyaḥ pravāha-rūpeṇa muñcantyaḥ, tas tāḥ pūrvoktāḥ sarvā anirvacanīyā vā, vrajasya priyāḥ kathāḥ, śrī-kṛṣṇasya bālya-krīḍādi-vṛttāni, sasmita-madhurāvaloka-pūrvaka-sanarmālāpān veti sarvathātyanta-śoka-vilāpa uktaḥ | yad vā, vatsa-bakādi-vadha-rūpāḥ kathāḥ kathayantyaḥ--"tādṛśā mahā-duṣṭāḥ kṛṣṇena hatāḥ | ayaṁ sarpaḥ ko nāma varākaḥ ? enaṁ hatvādhunaivāyāsyati ?" iti tan mātṛ-sāntvanārtham iti bhāvaḥ | viśeṣatas tāsāṁ śokoktiḥ śrī-viṣṇu-purāṇe yathā—

sarvā yaśodayā sārdhaṁ viśāmo’tra mahā-hrade |

nāga-rājasya no gantum asmākaṁ yujyate vraje ||

divasaḥ ko vinā sūryaṁ vinā candreṇa kā niśā |

vinā vṛṣeṇa kā gāvo vinā kṛṣṇena ko vrajaḥ ||

vinākṛtā na yāsyāmaḥ kṛṣnenānena gokulam |

araṇyaṁ nāpi sevyaṁ ca vāri-hīnaṁ yathā saraḥ ||

yatra nendīvara-dala-prakhya-kāntir ayaṁ hariḥ |

tenāpi mātur vāsena ratir astīti vismayaḥ ||

utphulla-paṅkaja-dala-spaṣṭa-kānti-vilocanam |

apaśyantyo hariṁ dīnāḥ kathaṁ goṣṭhe bhaviṣyatha ?

atyartha-madhurālāpa-hṛtāśeṣa-mano-dhanāḥ |

na vinā puṇḍarīkākṣaṁ yāsyāmo nanda-gokulam ||

bhogenāviṣṭitasyāpi sarpa-rājasya paśyata |

smito-śobhi-mukhaṁ gopyaḥ kṛṣṇasyāsmad-vilokane || [vi.pu 5.7.25-31]iti|

kṛṣṇānane’rpita-dṛśaś ca satyaḥ, āsan tat-sandarśanārthaṁ sthitā śokākulatayā bhūmāv upaviṣṭā vā | paścāc ca mṛtaka-pratīkā babhūvuḥ |

yad vā, kathayantyaḥ kṛṣṇānanārpita-dṛśaḥ satyo mṛtaka-pratīkā āsan babhūvuḥ, mohaṁ gatā ity arthaḥ | etā hi prāyo vrajeśvarī-sama-vayaskāḥ, pūrvoktāś ca prāyo nava-vayaskā bhagat-priyatamā iti kecid vivecayanti ||21||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : nanu, tan-mātā hanta kīdṛśī jātā ? ity apekṣāyāṁ śoka-bhareṇa kiñcid eva prakāśayan sarvāsām eva tāsāṁ daśā-viśeṣam āha—tā iti | tāḥ pūrvoktāḥ śrī-yaśodā-sakhyo’nyāḥ śrī-gopyaḥ | na patati kasminn api duḥkhe kulaṁ yasmāt, tad apatyaṁ parama-sneha-pātra-putram ity arthaḥ | ata eva tad anu lakṣīkṛtya tad-arthaṁ prakarṣeṇa sarvato’dhikatayā taptām | "praviṣṭām" iti pāṭhe hradaṁ praveṣṭum ārabdhām ity arthaḥ | tulya-vyathā api sa samyak anu nirantaraṁ gṛhītvā dhṛtvā śucaḥ śokāśrūṇi sravantyaḥ pravāha-rūpeṇa muñcantyaḥ | tās tāḥ pūtanādito daiva-kṛta-rakṣā-mayīḥ vatsa-bakādi-vadha-rūpās tavaiśvarya-mayīś ca vrajasya priya-kathāḥ kathayantyaḥ, "tādṛśā mahā-duṣṭā bahavo’pi hatāḥ, ayaṁ sarpaḥ teṣv ekaḥ ko nāma varākaḥ ? enaṁ hatvādhunaivāyāsyati" iti tan mātṛ-sāntvanārtham ity arthaḥ | tathā kṛṣṇārpita-dṛśaś ca satya āsan, paścāt mṛtaka-tulyāś cāsann ity arthaḥ | viśeṣatas tāsāṁ śokoktiḥ śrī-viṣṇu-purāṇe—

sarvā yaśodayā sārdhaṁ viśāmo’tra mahāhṛde |

nāga-rājasya no gantum asmākaṁ yujyate vraje ||

divasaḥ ko vinā sūryaṁ vinā candreṇa kā niśā |

vinā vṛṣeṇa kā gāvo vinā kṛṣṇena ko vrajaḥ ||

vinā kṛtā nā yāsyāmaḥ kṛṣṇenānena gokulam |

aramyaṁ nātisevyaṁ ca vāri-hīnaṁ yathā saraḥ ||

yatra nendīvara-dala-prakhya-kāntir ayaṁ hariḥ |

tenāpi mātarvāsena ratir astīti vismayaḥ ||

utphulla-paṅkaja-dala-spaṣṭa-kānti-vilocanam |

apaśyantyo hariṁ dīnāḥ kathaṁ goṣṭhe bhaviṣyathaḥ ||

atyanta-madhurālāpa-hatāśeṣa-mano-dhanāḥ |

na vinā puṇḍarīkākṣaṁ yāsyāmo nanda-gokulam ||

bhogenāveṣṭitasyāpi sarpa-rājasya paśyata |

smita-śobhi-mukhaṁ gopyaḥ kṛṣṇasyāsmad-vilokane || iti ||21||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : tāḥ pūrva-pūrvoktāḥ śrī-yaśodā-sakhyo’nyāḥ | tās tāḥ pūtanādibhyo rakṣā-mayīḥ ||21.22||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : tatra vātsalyavatīnāṁ vaiklavyam āha—tāḥ prasiddhāḥ purandhryaḥ apatyam anu lakṣīkṛtya prataptāṁ santāpa-jarjarāṁ | “praviṣṭam” iti pāṭhe apatya eva līnatāṁ prāptāṁ mūrcchitām iti yāvat | kṛṣṇa-mātaraṁ yaśodāṁ samyag anugṛhya ity adhunāpy asyāḥ śarīre prāṇāḥ prāyo vartante, tad idaṁ nopekṣaṇīyam iti tad-bhujābhyām aṅgīkṛtya śītala-salilenāśru-kalā-klinnaṁ mukhaṁ muhur muhuḥ prakṣālya vraja-priyasya kṛṣṇasya kathās tās tāḥ uccaiḥ kathayantyaḥ tac-cetanā-prāpanārtham iti bhāvaḥ | tāḥ kīdṛśyaḥ? śucaḥ śokasya sravantyo nadyaḥ sravantī nimnagāpagāḥ ity amaraḥ | sva-taraṅgenānyānapi plāvayantya iti bhāvaḥ | ante tu mṛtakasyeva pratīkāḥ avayavāḥ yāsāṁ tāḥ ||21||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : vatsalānāṁ daśām āha—tā iti | tā mātṛ-bhāvena prasiddhā gopyaḥ kṛṣṇa-mātaraṁ śrī-yaśodāṁ, apatyaṁ kṛṣṇam anu praviṣṭāṁ tad-antikaṁ praveṣṭuṁ kṛtārambhāṁ bhujābhyāṁ samanugṛhya tat-tulya-vyathāḥ satyaḥ śucaḥ śokāśrūtṇi sravantyo muñcantyaḥ vraja-priyasya kṛṣṇasya tās tā agha-vimardanādikāḥ kathāḥ kathayantyaḥ agham ivaitam api sarpaṁ vimardya sūnur āyātīti bodhayantya āsan | asmāsu viṣa-vipluṣṭāsu sa kathaṁ syāt ? iti bhāvaḥ | mṛtakasya iva pratīkā aṅgāni yāsāṁ tāḥ ||21||

...

|| 10.16.22 ||

kṛṣṇa-prāṇān nirviśato nandādīn vīkṣya taṁ hradam |

pratyaṣedhat sa bhagavān rāmaḥ kṛṣṇānubhāva-vit ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : tad dhradam kāliya-hradam | sa prasiddhaḥ | pratyaṣedhad iti—bho ārya-pādāḥ anena sarva-durgāṇi yūyam añjas tariṣyatha [bhā.pu. 10.8.16] iti śrī-garga-vacanād asya tv etādṛśa-durgottaraṇaṁ kiṁ citram iti vicārya vivekaṁ bhajata yuṣmāsu hradaṁ praviṣṭe paścāt svasty-āgatasyāsya mad-anujasya lālana-pālanādikaṁ kaiḥ kartavyam ? gopāyasva samāhitaḥ [bhā.pu. 10.8.19] iti garga-mahiṣi-nideśa-laṅghane pravṛttāḥ kathaṁ svely-ādi-vākyair ity arthaḥ |

nanu, teṣāṁ sarveṣāṁ pratiṣedhaṁ kartuṁ sa kathaṁ śaktas tatrāha—bhagavān sarva-śakti-yuktaḥ | kāṁścid uktaṁ yuktyā kāṁścid balena kāṁścid antaḥpreraṇayā ca | ata eva sarva-ramaṇād rāmaḥ |

nanu, so’pi kathaṁ svasyas tatrāha—kṛṣṇasya sarva-duḥkha-karṣakasya sadānanda-mūrte'nubhāva-vaibhavaṁ duṣṭa-dalanādi-rūpaṁ vetti iti toṣiṇī ||23||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : kathañcin mohopaśame kāla-vilambe ca hradaṁ niḥśeṣeṇa nirviśeṣeṇa vā praviśato nandādīn sarvān eva vraja-janān, sa vraja-rakṣārthaṁ bhagavatā gṛhe rakṣitā yaḥ, bandhu-vatsalatvena prasiddho vā |

nanu, teṣāṁ pratiṣedhanaṁ sa kathaṁ kartuṁ śaktaḥ ? tatrāha—bhagavān sarva-śakti-yuktaḥ—kāṁścid yuktyuktyā, kāṁścid balena, kāṁśic ca śrī-viṣṇu-purāṇānusāreṇa smita-śobhi-mukhatva-dṛṣṭyā bhagavat-svasthatādi-pradarśanenety arthaḥ | evaṁ sarva-ramaṇād rāmaḥ |

nanu, so’pi rāmaḥ kutaḥ svastha āsīt ? tatrāha—kṛṣṇasya para-brahma-mūrter bhagavato ’nubhāvaṁ prabhāvaṁ vetti iti tathā saḥ | -bharodayena mohitā bhūmau nipatyāsann iti jñeyam—upalabhyotthitāḥ sarve [bhā.pu 10.17.14] iti vakṣyamāṇatvāt ||22||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : kathañcin mohopaśame kāla-vilambe ca hradaṁ niḥśeṣeṇa praviśataḥ nandādīn sarvān eva vraja-janān saḥ vraja-rakṣārthaṁ bhagavatā gṛhe tyakto yaḥ bandhu-vatsalatvena prasiddho vā |

nanu, teṣāṁ sarveṣāṁ pratiṣedhanaṁ sa kathaṁ kartuṁ śaktaḥ ? tatrāha—bhagavān sarva-śakti-yuktaḥ kāṁścid yukta-yuktyā, kāṁścid balena, kāṁścid antaḥ-preraṇayā ca | evaṁ sarva-ramaṇād rāmaḥ |

nanu, so’pi nāma kutaḥ svastha āsīt ? tatrāha—kṛṣṇasya para-brahma-mūrter bhagavataḥ anubhāvaṁ prabhāvaṁ vetti iti tathā saḥ ||23||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : pratyaṣedhad iti "bho āryapādāḥ ! anena sarva-durgāṇi yūyam añjas tariṣyatha [bhā.pu. 10.8.16] iti garga-vacanād asya tv etādṛśa-durgottaraṇaṁ kiṁ citram ? iti vicārya vivekaṁ bhajata | yuṣmāsu hradaṁ praviṣṭeṣu paścāt svasty-āgatasyāsya mad-anujasya lālana-pālanādikaṁ kaiḥ kartavyaṁ ? gopāyasva samāhitaḥ [bhā.pu. 10.8.19] iti garga-maharṣi-nirdeśa-laṅghane pravṛttāḥ kathaṁ stha ?" ity-ādi-vākyair ity arthaḥ | bhagavān iti tatra sāmarthyam ||23||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : pratyaṣedhad iti | "bho ārya-caraṇāḥ ! anena sarva-durgāṇi yūyam añjas tariṣyatha, gopāyasva samāhitaḥ [bhā.pu. 10.8.16,19] iti gargācāryair uktaṁ kiṁ vismaratha ? tac ca kāliya-durga-taraṇam asya mahā-puruṣasya mad-anujasya kiyat ? daṣṭaś cāsakṛd asya prabhāvo bhavac-caraṇe yuṣmāsu viṣa-vahni-vipluṣṭeṣu ko’sya lālana-kṛd iti tāny avārayat" ity arthaḥ ||23||

...

|| 10.16.23 ||

itthaṁ sva-gokulam ananya-gatiṁ nirīkṣya

sa-strī-kumāram atiduḥkhitam ātma-hetoḥ |

ājñāya martya-padavīm anuvartamānaḥ

sthitvā muhūrtam udatiṣṭhad uraṅga-bandhāt ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : ananya-gatim ātmānam itthaṁ nirīkṣya, ata eva ātma-hetoḥ sva-gokulam atiduḥkhitam ājñāya uraṅga-bandhād udatiṣṭhad ity anvayaḥ ||23||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : ittham sarpa-veṣṭitam | nānyo gatis trātā yasyāsau’nanya-gatiḥ tam | sarva-rakṣakatve sati sarvārakṣya nirīkṣya vimṛśya | ata eva ananya-gatitvād eva | ātma-hetoḥ mad-artham | uraṅga-bandhād ahi-veṣṭanāt | udatiṣṭhat pṛthag babhūva | gokula-pakṣe tv amo lug-abhāva ārṣaḥ | sakalāḥ striyaḥ kumārāḥ sa-strī-kumāram iti sākalye’vyayībhāvaḥ | muhūrtaṁ stabdhaṁ iva sthitvā, "kiṁ re kāliya ! tvayā vikrama-sarvasvaṁ darśito'haṁ samprati gopāla-bālako’py ayaṁ vikrama-lavaṁ darśayati, paśya !" ity uktvā uraga-bandhād udatiṣṭhat pṛthag bhavitum udyato’bhūt ||23||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : ājñāya martya-padavīm ity-ādi | martya-padavīm ājñāya jñātvā, "ete martyā atikṣaṇa-bhaṅgura-prāṇā asmad-anālokena mariṣyanty eva, tad alaṁ vilambena" ity anuvartamāno vicārayan muhūrtaṁ sthitvā uraga-bandhād udatiṣṭhat ||23||

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : ittham anena teṣāṁ sarveṣām api mohādinā prakāreṇa | na vidyate’nyaḥ patiḥ rakṣako yasya tathā-bhūtam ātmānam iti śeṣaḥ | udatiṣthat śrī-kṛṣṇaḥ | anyat tair vyāñjitam |

yad vā, ittham uktenānena śoka-mohādy-anuparama-prakāreṇa atiduḥkhitam, anyathā ekāntinas teṣāṁ patiṁ cātmānaṁ nirīkṣya ālocyety "eṣāṁ rakṣā avaśyam evāśu kāryā" iti bhāvaḥ | viśeṣataś ca ātma-hetor eva atiduḥkhitaṁ svakīyam akṛtyenāpi vakṣyaṁ gokulam ājñāya svabhāvaḥ para-duḥkhāsahiṣṇutā bhāty eva | tatra ca svakīyānāṁ tatrāpy ātma-hetukam ity utthāne’titvarā bodhitā | sa-strī-kumāram iti śrī-yaśodādīnāṁ gopīnāṁ śrīdāmādi-sahacara-gopa-kumārāṇāṁ ca duḥkha-viśeṣa-vivakṣayā pṛthaṅ-nirdiṣṭam |

athavā, ittham ukta-prakārakaṁ paramārtaṁ maraṇodyataṁ ca sva-gokula-svāminam | kiṁ vā, anyathā gopās teṣāṁ patiṁ śrī-nandaṁ sa-strī-kumāram ātma-hetor atiduḥkhitam ājñaya | he ananya ! iti śrī-parīkṣitaṁ prati sambodhanaṁ vā—tava iva ananyānāṁ teṣām api prāṇa-rakṣā-pūrvakāśeṣa-maṅgalaṁ tenāvaśyaṁ kṛtyam eveti bhāvaḥ | tathāpi patiṁ gokulasyety arthāt tam eva | anyat samānam |

martya-padavīm anuvartamāno laukika-līlāyāṁ darśayann ity arthaḥ | ato muhūrtam alpa-kālam evoraga-bhoga-bandhe sthitvā | yad vā, yaṁ prati niḥsāraṇādi-daṇḍo vidhīyate, tasya doṣo lokeṣu darśyata itīdṛśīṁ martya-padavīm anusarann api śīghram udatiṣṭhad ity arthaḥ | bhāva-viśeṣāliṅganādinā yo hi milati, sa balān na mocyate, kintu kṣaṇaṁ tena saṅgatya sthīyata iti loka-rītim anusarann iti tattvārthaḥ ||23||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : ittham anena sarveṣāṁ teṣām api mohanādinā prakāreṇa na vidyate anyā gatiḥ rakṣako yasya tathā-bhūtam ātmānam iti śeṣaḥ | "patim" iti pāṭhe sa evārthaḥ | udatiṣṭhat śrī-kṛṣṇaḥ | anyat taiḥ |

yad vā, sa ca muhūrtaṁ sthitvā uraṅgama-bandhād udatiṣṭhat | muhūrta-sthitau hetuḥ--martya-padavīṁ yaṁ prati daṇḍo vidhīyate tasya doṣo loke darśyate itīdṛśīṁ tan nītim anuvartamāna iti | utthāne hetuḥ--svam ātmīyaṁ gokulam itthaṁ nijotthānaṁ vinā na jīviṣyatīti prakāreṇa na vidyate’nyā gatiḥ rakṣako yasya | kiṁ vā, na vidyate’nyā yatrāhi-veṣṭane svasyāvasthitiḥ, tasmād aparā gatir gamanaṁ yasyeti, tatraiva praveśa-niścayo yasyety arthaḥ | tādṛśaṁ nirīkṣya tac ceṣṭā-darśanena niścitya | amo lug-abhāvaḥ ārṣaḥ | tatrāpi sa-strī-kumāraṁ kṛtsnam ity arthaḥ, sākalye’vyayībhāvaḥ | ceṣṭā-darśanam evāha—ātma-hetor atiduḥkhitaṁ duḥkha-parākāṣṭhām āpannaṁ samyak jñātveti svabhāvato jana-mātrasya duḥkhā-sahiṣṇutā tasmin vartata eva | tatrāpi svīyasya tatrāpy ātmānavāpti-hetuka-duḥkhasya | tatrāpy ātma-duḥkha-duḥkhitasyeti krama-jñānenotthāneti tvarā bodhitā evaṁ tad-utthānādikā sarvaiva līlā-vraja-janena dṛṣṭeti gamyate ||23||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ) : sa ca muhūrtaṁ sthitvā uraga-bandhād udatiṣṭhat | tāvat sthitau hetuḥ--martya-padavīm anuvartamānaḥ | utthāne hetuḥ--svam ātmīyam itthaṁ gokulaṁ nijotthānaṁ vinā na jīviṣyatīti prakāreṇa na vidyate’nyaḥ patiḥ rakṣako yasya tādṛśaṁ nirīkṣya dṛṣṭvā, tathāpi sa-strī-kumāraṁ śrī-kṛṣṇam ity arthaḥ | prakāram evāha—ātma-ity-ādi ||23||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : atha teṣām eva karuṇa-rasa-parākāṣṭhām ālakṣya kadācid amī mad-viyogam āśaṅkya naśyeran, tad atyasamañjasaṁ bhaviṣyatīti tāṁ līlāṁ sajihīrṣur yad akarot tad āha—itthaṁ sva-gokulam ity-ādi | ete martya-prāyā mad-viyogena mariṣyanty eveti teṣāṁ martya-padavīm ājñāya jñātvā kāliyena saha tāṁ pūrvoktāṁ khelām anuvartamānaḥ san muhūrtaṁ sthitvā uraṅga-bandhanāt urage yaḥ sva-kṛto bandhaḥ, na tu tat-kartṛko bandhaḥ, tasmād udatiṣṭhat, svādhīna-bandhatvād icchā-mātreṇaivotthānam | anyat samam ||23||

———————————————————————————————————————

viśvanātha-cakravartī (sārārtha-darśiṇī) : ananya-gatim iti puṁs tvam ārṣam | ājñāya samyak jñātvā muhūrtaṁ ghaṭikā-dvayaṁ stabdha iva sthitvā, "kiṁ re kāliya! tvayā vikrama-sarvasvam ahaṁ darśita iva, samprati gopa-bālako’py ayaṁ vikrama-lavaṁ darśayati | paśya !" ity uktvā uraṅga uragaḥ, tad-bandhāt udatiṣṭhat ||23||

———————————————————————————————————————

baladeva-vidyabhūṣaṇaḥ (vaiṣṇavānandinī) : kāliyasyotsāha-vardhanāya vrajaukasāṁ bhāva-śobhā-darśanāya ca nirīhatāṁ prakāśyedānīṁ vikramaṁ prakāśayad ity āha—ittham iti | sarveṣāṁ mohādinā prakāreṇa na anyo gatir yasya, tat puṁstvaṁ chāndasaṁ | ātma-hetor atiduḥkhitam ājñāya martya-padavīṁ yaṁ prati daṇḍo vidheyas tad-doṣo loke dṛśya iti nītim anuvartamāno ’nusaran muhūrtaṁ sthitvā uraṅgasya bandhād udatiṣṭhat nirgataḥ ||23||

...

|| 10.16.24 ||

tat-prathyamāna-vapuṣā vyathitātma-bhogas

tyaktvonnamayya kupitaḥ sva-phaṇān bhujaṅgaḥ |

tasthau śvasan chvasana-randhra-viṣāmbarīṣa-

stabdhekṣaṇolmuka-mukho harim īkṣamāṇaḥ ||

śrīdhara-svāmī (bhāvārtha-dīpikā) : tasya prathyamānena vapuṣā vyathitātma-śarīro bhujaṅgaḥ kuṇḍalīm unmucya taṁ tyaktvā kupitaḥ sva-phaṇān unnamayya śvasan kevalam īkṣamāṇas tasthau | kathaṁ-bhūtaḥ ? śvasana-randhreṣu nāsā-vivareṣu viṣaṁ yasya sa tathā, ambarīṣo maṇḍaka-pāka-bhājanaṁ tadvat santaptāni stabdhāni īkṣaṇāni yasya sa tathā, ulmukāni mukheṣu yasya, sa ca sa ca sa ca ||24||

———————————————————————————————————————

vaṁśīdharaḥ (bhāvārtha-dīpikā-prakāśaḥ) : prathyamānena baddhamānena | meḍakaṁ sūkṣma-rāṭike iti śāśvataḥ | taptāya prasiddham | yad vā, ambarīṣo bhrāṣṭram anna-bharjanārthaṁ racito gṛhakāra-yantraḥ klībo'mbarīṣaṁ bhrāṣṭro na ity amaraḥ ||24||

———————————————————————————————————————

śrīnātha-cakravartī (caitanya-mata-mañjuṣā) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————

sanātana-gosvāmī (bṛhad-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : utthāna-prakāram eva darśayan kāliyasya glānim āha—tad iti | tasya kṛṣṇasya prathyamānena svayaṁ vistāryamāṇena kiñcid ucchvāsyamānena vapuṣā vyathitaḥ pīḍita ātma-bhogaḥ sva-dehaḥ | kiṁ vā, ātmano deho vā, bhogaś ca deho yasya saḥ | niviḍa-dṛḍha-veṣṭanaṁ gatasya bhogasya tad-antarvarti-śrī-vapuṣaḥ kiñcid utphullanenākṛṣṭyā troṭana-duḥkhotpatteḥ kupitatvād eva | kiṁ vā, vyathitātma-bhogatvāt śvasenaiva tad- atyantābhivyañjanāt ambarīṣo’tra viṣa-pāka-pātraṁ tad-upamayekṣaṇānām atiraktatvaṁ durviṣamayatvaṁ ca | ulmuka-mukhatvaṁ ca mukhato jvalad-viṣāgni-śikhā-mocanāt | eṣām uttarottaraṁ ghoratvam ūhyam | evaṁ pūrvoddiṣṭaṁ sarvato’tidurviṣamayatvaṁ darśitam | hariṁ durabhimāna-doṣa-haranān nijāvāsa-haraṇodyamād vā |

tattvārthas tu avyathitātma-bhogas tvarayotthānāt praṇayena kupito manoharatvāt tam īkṣamāṇa eva tasthau | śvāsādayaḥ sāttvika-vikārā eva | tatra viṣāmbarīṣatvādikaṁ jāti-svābhāvikam eveti ||24||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (laghu-vaiṣṇava-toṣaṇī) : utthāna-prakāram eva darśayan kāliyasya glānim āha—tad iti | tasya kṛṣṇasya prathyamānena svayaṁ vistāryamāṇena kiñcid ucchvāsyamānena vapuṣā vyathitaḥ truṭyann iva pīḍitaḥ ātma-bhogo yasya | ātma-śabdena tatrātyantam adhyāsaṁ saṁvyajya pīḍā vaiśiṣṭyaṁ dyotitaṁ | sva-śabdaś cāsādhāraṇatayā vivakṣayā | ambarīṣam atra jvalad-viṣa-bharjana-pātraṁ hariṁ durabhimāna-doṣa-haraṇāt nijāvāsa-haraṇodyamatvād vā | anyat taiḥ | tatra kuṇḍalaṁ veṣṭanam ||24||

———————————————————————————————————————

jīva-gosvāmī (krama-sandarbhaḥ, bṛhat-krama-sandarbhaḥ) : na vyākhyātam.

———————————————————————————————————————
